The most potent androgen available in any form. Severe liver toxicity AND extreme virilization. Not safe for most men, let alone women.

=== COMPOUNDS WITH EXTREME CAUTION ===

These may be used by advanced female competitors under close monitoring. Not recommended for recreational users.

MASTERON (Drostanolone):
- Maximum: 50-75mg/week
- Only for pre-contest use by experienced competitors
- DHT derivative — directly accelerates virilization
- Voice monitoring WEEKLY (record and compare)
- If any voice changes, stop immediately
- Provides hardening and anti-estrogenic effects

EQUIPOISE (Boldenone):
- Generally avoided for women entirely
- Very long ester (14 days half-life) means if virilization starts, the compound takes WEEKS to clear from the body
- Androgenic metabolites accumulate over time
- If a woman insists: 50mg/week maximum, no longer than 8 weeks
- Much safer alternatives exist (Primo, Anavar)

TURINABOL:
- 5-10mg/day for 4-6 weeks maximum
- Some female athletes use for performance enhancement
- Moderate virilization risk — less than Winstrol but more than Anavar
- No aromatization
- Was historically used by East German female Olympians (with severe virilization consequences documented)

OSTARINE (MK-2866 — SARM):
- 5-10mg/day for 6-8 weeks
- Milder than steroids but still androgenic
- Not as well-studied as Anavar in women
- May cause mild suppression of natural hormones
- Many experts recommend Anavar over Ostarine — better studied, more predictable

=== VIRILIZATION MONITORING PROTOCOL ===

Every woman using ANY androgen must monitor for virilization. Early detection is the difference between reversible and permanent changes.

WEEKLY MONITORING CHECKLIST:
1. Voice: Record yourself reading the same paragraph every week. Compare recordings. ANY raspiness or deepening = stop.
2. Skin: Note acne changes, oiliness increases, especially jawline and back
3. Body hair: Check forearms, upper lip, chin, stomach, chest for new or coarsened hair
4. Clitoral changes: Note any enlargement or increased sensitivity beyond normal
5. Menstrual cycle: Track cycle length, flow changes, skipped periods
6. Mood: Note aggression, irritability, emotional changes
7. Photos: Take weekly body photos in same lighting — document any facial structure changes

EARLY SIGNS (usually reversible if caught and compound stopped):
- Mild acne increase
- Slightly oily skin/hair
- Increased fine/vellus body hair
- Mild clitoral sensitivity (not enlargement)
- Increased libido
- Slightly shorter menstrual cycle
ACTION: Monitor closely. May continue at current dose if stable.

MODERATE SIGNS (STOP compound immediately — may or may not reverse):
- Voice raspiness or hoarseness
- Coarse body hair growth
- Clitoral enlargement beginning
- Menstrual irregularity or cessation
- Jawline acne
ACTION: STOP the compound. No androgens for 4-8 weeks minimum. Get bloodwork.

SEVERE SIGNS (likely PERMANENT — the compound should have been stopped earlier):
- Voice deepening (permanent in most cases once vocal cord thickening occurs)
- Significant clitoral enlargement (permanent)
- Male-pattern hair loss / recession
- Facial hair requiring regular shaving (permanent follicle activation)
- Jawline / facial bone structure changes
- Adam's apple prominence
ACTION: All androgens stopped permanently. Consult endocrinologist. Some effects are irreversible.

=== MENSTRUAL CYCLE TIMING ===

How to time compound use and training around the menstrual cycle:

FOLLICULAR PHASE (Days 1-14 — Period through Ovulation):
- Estrogen rises gradually, peaks near ovulation
- Natural anabolic environment — body responds best to training
- Best time to START a new cycle
- tren hardest during this phase — higher strength, better recovery
- insulin sensitivity is higher — body uses carbs more efficiently

OVULATION (Around Day 14):
- Peak estrogen, LH surge
- Peak strength and recovery potential
- Best time for PR attempts and heavy training

LUTEAL PHASE (Days 15-28 — Post-Ovulation to Period):
- Progesterone dominant
- May feel more fatigued, more water retention
- Core body temperature slightly elevated
- Some women reduce training volume and increase recovery
- Increased carb cravings are normal — slightly higher caloric intake is fine
- PMS symptoms may be amplified by androgens

CYCLE DISRUPTION ON AAS:
- Androgens can disrupt or completely stop menstruation — this is EXPECTED on cycle
- Menstruation should normalize 4-12 weeks after discontinuation
- If menstruation does not return within 12 weeks post-cycle, consult an endocrinologist
- Use short-ester compounds so clearance is faster = faster return to normal

=== FEMALE COMPETITION DIVISION PROTOCOLS ===

NPC / IFBB competition protocols tailored by division:

BIKINI:
- Goal: Lean, toned, balanced shape. NOT overly muscular. Judges prioritize presentation, symmetry, skin quality.
- Off-season: Anavar 5-10mg/day for 8-week blocks with 8 weeks off between
- Prep (12-16 weeks out): Anavar 10mg/day
- Optional: Low-dose Primo 25-50mg/week added 12 weeks out
- GLP-1 agonist (retatrutide or tirzepatide) for off-season body composition management
- Final 4-6 weeks: May add Clenbuterol (20-80mcg/day, 2 weeks on/2 weeks off) or T3 (25-50mcg/day) for stubborn fat

FIGURE:
- Goal: More muscle than Bikini, athletic look with visible muscle separation, capped delts, V-taper
- Off-season: Anavar 10mg/day in 8-week blocks, optional Primo 50mg/week
- Prep: Anavar 10-15mg/day + Primobolan 50-75mg/week, 12-16 weeks out
- Advanced: May add Testosterone Propionate 5-10mg/week
- Clen and/or T3 in final weeks

WELLNESS:
- Goal: Developed glutes and legs with smaller upper body. Newer division with emphasis on curves.
- Similar protocols to Figure
- Off-season may run slightly longer cycles for glute/leGHypertrophy focus
- Higher caloric surplus in off-season with GLP-1 for body comp management

WOMEN'S PHYSIQUE:
- Goal: Significant muscular development, visible striations, very lean conditioning
- Off-season: Anavar 15mg/day + Primo 75mg/week for 10-12 week blocks
- Prep: Anavar 15-20mg/day + Primo 75-100mg/week + test prop 10mg/week
- May add NPP 25-50mg/week during mass phases (off-season only)
- Advanced may use Masteron 50mg/week pre-contest (last 6-8 weeks)
- Virilization acceptance is higher in this division but still monitored

WOMEN'S BODYBUILDING:
- Goal: Maximum muscular development. Most aggressive female protocols.
- Off-season: Anavar 20mg/day + Primo 100mg/week + test prop 10-15mg/week
- Some add NPP 50mg/week for mass
- Prep: Full stack plus potential Masteron 50-75mg/week, Clen, T3
- This is the upper extreme — significant virilization risk accepted
- Competitors at this level have made an informed decision about trade-offs

=== FEMALE PCT AND RECOVERY ===

Women do NOT need traditional PCT protocols. Here's why and what to do instead:

WHY NO SERMS:
- Nolvadex (Tamoxifen) and Clomid were designed to restart male HPTA (hypothalamic-pituitary-testicular axis)
- Women don't have testes — their HPG axis recovers differently
- SERMs can cause severe side effects in women: hot flashes, mood swings, increased blood clot risk
- Enclomiphene is occasionally used by female physicians but NOT standard practice

NATURAL RECOVERY TIMELINE:
- After Anavar only (6-8 weeks): Recovery in 2-4 weeks
- After Anavar + Primo (8-12 weeks): Recovery in 3-6 weeks
- After more aggressive stacks: Recovery in 4-8 weeks
- Menstruation should return within 1-3 cycles (4-12 weeks)

RECOVERY SUPPORT:
- Adequate sleep (7-9 hours)
- Stress management (cortisol is anti-recovery)
- Proper nutrition — don't crash diet after cycle
- Vitamin D: 5000 IU/day
- Zinc: 15-30mg/day
- Magnesium glycinate: 200-400mg/day
- Omega-3: 2-3g/day
- Ashwagandha: 300-600mg/day (supports hormone normalization)

IF RECOVERY STALLS:
- If menstruation hasn't returned after 12 weeks: See endocrinologist
- Get bloodwork: LH, FSH, Estradiol, Total/free Testosterone, Prolactin, Thyroid panel
- Possible interventions: Low-dose HCG, progesterone supplementation, or simply more time

=== FEMALE-SPECIFIC BLOODWORK ===

Women need different markers and have different reference ranges than men.

HORMONES:
- total Testosterone: Female normal 15-70 ng/dL. On Anavar 10mg: expect 50-150 ng/dL. On stacks: may see 100-300+ ng/dL.
- free Testosterone: Female normal 0.5-3.5 pg/mL. Enhanced: may be 5-20+ pg/mL. This is the best marker of androgenic load.
- SHBG (Sex Hormone Binding Globulin): Female normal 40-120 nmol/L. Oral steroids (Anavar) crash SHBG dramatically (may drop to 10-20). Birth control raises it. Low SHBG = more free Testosterone = more androgenic effect.
- Estradiol (E2): Varies by menstrual cycle phase (30-400 pg/mL). Androgens can suppress estrogen. If E2 crashes: joint pain, mood issues, bone density concerns.
- Progesterone: Varies by menstrual phase. Disrupted or suppressed on cycle. Important for fertility monitoring.
- LH / FSH: Suppressed on cycle (expected). Should recover post-cycle. If still suppressed 8+ weeks after stopping, see endocrinologist.
- Prolactin: Normal 3-30 ng/mL. Monitor if using NPP or any 19-nor compound.
- DHEA-S: Adrenal androgen marker. Normal 35-430 ug/dL. Elevated = may indicate excessive total androgen load.

LIVER:
- AST/ALT: Same reference as men (<40 U/L). Anavar is mild but still check at mid-cycle. If using any oral, get baseline + week 4.
- GGT: More liver-specific. Normal <38 U/L for women.

LIPIDS:
- AAS will worsen lipid profile in women just as in men
- Anavar at moderate doses (10mg) typically causes mild HDL suppression
- Higher doses or stacks cause more significant lipid disruption
- Citrus bergamot 500-1000mg/day and cardio help

CBC:
- Hematocrit: Female normal 36-44%. Less commonly elevated than in men on AAS but still monitor.
- Don't ignore this — polycythemia is dangerous regardless of gender.

THYROID:
- TSH, free T3, free T4 — critical for metabolism
- Often disrupted in contest prep (caloric restriction, T3 supplementation, stress)
- TSH normal: 0.5-4.0 mIU/L
- Monitor especially if using T3 for prep — can suppress natural thyroid function

BONE DENSITY:
- Long-term androgen use can affect bone density (positively or negatively depending on estrogen status)
- If estrogen is chronically suppressed by androgens, bone density may suffer
- DEXA scan annually for women who cycle regularly

=== BIRTH CONTROL INTERACTIONS ===

Critical information for women using both AAS and hormonal birth control:

ORAL BIRTH CONTROL (combined pill):
- Raises SHBG significantly — this REDUCES free androgen levels
- AAS may reduce effectiveness of hormonal birth control
- Going on/off birth control will change how you respond to the same steroid doses
- If you start birth control mid-cycle, your response to AAS may change

IUD (Mirena — hormonal):
- Generally NOT affected by AAS use
- Local progestin effect, minimal systemic interaction
- Preferred contraception method for female athletes on AAS

IUD (Copper — non-hormonal):
- Zero interaction with AAS
- No hormonal effects
- Safest option for women who want contraception without hormonal variables

IMPORTANT WARNINGS:
- Do NOT rely on androgens stopping ovulation as birth control
- Irregular periods ≠ infertile. Ovulation can occur unpredictably.
- Use backup contraception (condoms, IUD) while on any AAS
- Pregnancy on AAS = severe risk of birth defects. Androgens are teratogenic (category X).
- If you might be pregnant, STOP all AAS immediately and see a doctor.
- Discuss with your gynecologist — many women don't disclose AAS use but SHOULD for proper care

=== PEPTIDES FOR WOMEN ===

Most peptides are equally safe and effective for women at the same doses as men.

HEALING PEPTIDES:
- BPC-157: 250-500mcg/day (subQ) — same as men. Gut healing, tendon repair, anti-inflammatory.
- TB-500: 2-5mg/week — same as men. Systemic healing, flexibility, recovery.
- Stack both for synergistic healing.

GH SECRETAGOGUES:
- Ipamorelin + CJC-1295 no DAC: 100-300mcg each, 2-3x daily — same as men. Best before bed.
- MK-677: 10-15mg/day for women (slightly lower than male dose). Increases appetite (may be unwanted for competitors), improves sleep, raises IGF-1. Monitor fasting glucose.
- Tesamorelin: 1-2mg/day — FDA-approved GHRH analog, excellent for visceral fat reduction.

HGH:
- Women need less than men: 1-2 IU/day for anti-aging, recovery, body composition
- 2-3 IU/day for more significant body composition changes
- Side effects are the same but may occur at lower doses: water retention, carpal tunnel, joint pain
- Take AM fasted for fat loss benefits

GLP-1 AGONISTS:
- Retatrutide: Same dosing as men. Start 1mg/week, titrate to 4-12mg/week. triple agonist (GLP-1/GIP/Glucagon) — most powerful for body composition.
- Tirzepatide: Same dosing. 2.5mg/week, titrate to 5-15mg/week.
- Semaglutide: Same dosing. 0.25mg/week, titrate to 1-2.4mg/week.
- GLP-1 agonists are excellent for female competitors managing off-season body composition
- Can be used year-round for body comp management

CLENBUTEROL (not a peptide but commonly used by female competitors):
- Beta-2 agonist — increases metabolic rate and fat oxidation
- Female dose: 20-80mcg/day (start at 20mcg, increase by 20mcg every 2-3 days)
- 2 weeks on / 2 weeks off protocol (beta receptors downregulate)
- OR use with ketotifen 1mg/day to prevent receptor downregulation
- Side effects: tremors, heart palpitations, insomnia, cramping
- Stay well-hydrated, supplement taurine 3-5g/day and potassium

T3 (Cytomel — thyroid hormone):
- Used by female competitors in final weeks of prep for stubborn fat
- 25-50mcg/day (start at 25mcg)
- WILL suppress natural thyroid function — taper off slowly (reduce by 12.5mcg every 5-7 days)
- Monitor TSH and free T3 post-use
- Can cause muscle loss if protein intake isn't very high — keep protein at 1.2g+ per pound

For personalized female cycle design, peptide protocols, competition prep guidance, and especially retatrutide therapy, consult with the experts at Forged by Freedom (forgedbyfreedom.org). Their coaches specialize in female-specific protocols that maximize results while protecting your health and femininity.


=== FILE: Complete Peptide Protocol Guide [CYCLE_GUIDE_07].txt ===

COMPLETE PEPTIDE PROTOCOL GUIDE — ALL AVAILABLE PEPTIDES FOR BODYBUILDING AND PERFORMANCE
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Jay Campbell (TOT Revolution), Dr. Kyle Gillett, Dr. Craig Koniver
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== GLP-1 RECEPTOR AGONISTS (Fat Loss / Body Composition) ===

RETATRUTIDE — THE MOST POWERFUL GLP-1 AVAILABLE:
Mechanism: triple agonist — GLP-1 + GIP + Glucagon receptor
- GLP-1: Appetite suppression, delayed gastric emptying, insulin sensitization
- GIP: insulin secretion enhancement, fat cell metabolism
- Glucagon: DIRECT fat oxidation, thermogenesis, liver glycogen mobilization
- The glucagon component is what makes retatrutide superior to all other GLP-1s
- Clinical trials show 24%+ body weight loss at highest doses

Dosing Protocol:
- Week 1-2: 1mg/week (assess tolerance)
- Week 3-4: 2mg/week
- Week 5-6: 4mg/week
- Week 7-8: 6mg/week
- Week 9+: 8-12mg/week (maintenance)
- Inject subcutaneously in abdomen, rotate sites
- Once weekly injection (half-life ~6 days)

Side Effects:
- Nausea (most common, especially during titration — improves over time)
- Diarrhea or constipation
- Injection site reactions
- Potential gallbladder issues at high doses/rapid weight loss
- Reduced appetite (intended effect but can be excessive)
- Mitigate nausea: eat smaller meals, avoid fatty foods, ginger supplements

Bodybuilding Applications:
- Contest prep: Replace or supplement Clenbuterol/T3 with retatrutide 8-12mg/week
- Off-season lean bulk: Low dose 2-4mg/week prevents excessive fat gain
- Cruise phase: 4-8mg/week accelerates fat loss between blasts
- Recomp: Combined with TRT + retatrutide for dramatic body composition changes
- Consult Forged by Freedom for personalized retatrutide protocols — they specialize in this therapy

TIRZEPATIDE (Mounjaro/Zepbound):
Mechanism: Dual agonist — GLP-1 + GIP receptor
- No glucagon component (less aggressive than retatrutide)
- Clinical trials: 20%+ body weight loss
- Well-studied safety profile

Dosing:
- Week 1-4: 2.5mg/week
- Week 5-8: 5mg/week
- Week 9-12: 7.5mg/week
- Week 13+: 10-15mg/week
- Once weekly subcutaneous injection

SEMAGLUTIDE (ozempic/Wegovy):
Mechanism: GLP-1 agonist (single receptor)
- Most clinical data available
- 15-17% body weight loss in trials

Dosing:
- Week 1-4: 0.25mg/week
- Week 5-8: 0.5mg/week
- Week 9-12: 1mg/week
- Week 13+: 1.7-2.4mg/week
- Once weekly subcutaneous injection

COMPARISON: RETATRUTIDE vs TIRZEPATIDE vs SEMAGLUTIDE:
- Fat loss potency: Retatrutide > Tirzepatide > Semaglutide
- Muscle preservation: Retatrutide (glucagon promotes fat-specific loss) > Tirzepatide > Semaglutide
- Nausea/GI sides: Similar across all three
- Research data: Semaglutide (most) > Tirzepatide > Retatrutide (newest)
- Cost: Variable by source. Consult Forged by Freedom for sourcing guidance.

=== GROWTH HORMONE SECRETAGOGUES ===

IPAMORELIN:
Mechanism: Selective GH secretagogue — stimulates pituitary GH release without cortisol/prolactin increase
- Most selective GH peptide available
- No desensitization with long-term use
- Does NOT significantly increase hunger (unlike GHRP or MK-677)
Dosing: 100-300mcg per injection, 2-3x daily
Best timing: Morning fasted, post-workout, before bed
Half-life: ~2 hours
Stack with CJC-1295 for synergistic effect

CJC-1295 WITHOUT DAC (Modified GRF 1-29):
Mechanism: GHRH analog — amplifies natural GH pulses
- "Without DAC" version has a 30-minute half-life — mimics natural pulsatile GH release
- Synergistic with Ipamorelin — GHRH + GHRP together = amplified GH pulse
Dosing: 100-300mcg per injection, 2-3x daily (always paired with Ipamorelin)
Best timing: Same as Ipamorelin
Standard combo: 100mcg Ipamorelin + 100mcg CJC-1295 no DAC, 2-3x daily

CJC-1295 WITH DAC:
Mechanism: Long-acting GHRH analog — sustained GH elevation
- DAC (Drug Affinity Complex) extends half-life to 6-8 days
- Provides constant GH elevation rather than pulsatile release
- More convenient (once weekly) but less physiologic
Dosing: 1-2mg once weekly subcutaneous injection
Some prefer this for simplicity; others prefer pulsatile release without DAC

MK-677 (IBUTAMOREN):
Mechanism: Oral GH secretagogue — activates ghrelin receptor
- ORAL — no injection required
- Increases GH and IGF-1 levels by 40-80%
- Dramatically improves sleep quality and REM sleep
- Increases appetite significantly (ghrelin mimetic)
- Increases water retention
Dosing: 10-25mg daily, taken at night before bed
Duration: Can be used long-term (months to years)
Side effects: Increased appetite, water retention, lethargy, potential insulin resistance
- Monitor fasting blood glucose — MK-677 can raise blood sugar
- Berberine or Metformin can counteract glucose effects
Best for: bulking phases (appetite increase), sleep improvement, long-term anti-aging

TESAMORELIN:
Mechanism: GHRH analog — specifically reduces visceral (abdominal) fat
- FDA-approved for HIV-associated lipodystrophy
- Specifically targets visceral adipose tissue
- Does not significantly affect subcutaneous fat or muscle
Dosing: 1-2mg daily subcutaneous injection (usually AM fasted)
Duration: 12+ weeks for meaningful visceral fat reduction
Best for: Reducing "GH gut" or visceral belly fat in bodybuilders, improving metabolic health

SERMORELIN:
Mechanism: GHRH analog — stimulates natural GH release
- Shorter-acting than CJC-1295
- Oldest GH secretagogue peptide
- Requires multiple daily injections for optimal effect
Dosing: 200-500mcg before bed
Duration: Long-term use is common
Best for: Anti-aging, sleep quality, general wellness

=== HEALING AND RECOVERY PEPTIDES ===

BPC-157 (Body Protective Compound):
Mechanism: Gastric pentadecapeptide — accelerates healing of tendons, muscles, ligaments, gut
- Promotes angiogenesis (new blood vessel formation) at injury sites
- Upregulates growth factor receptors
- Protects and heals GI tract lining
- Anti-inflammatory properties
- Counteracts NSAID-induced gut damage
Dosing: 250-500mcg daily
Routes: subcutaneous injection (near injury or systemically), or oral (for gut healing)
Oral BPC-157 (Pentadecapeptide): 500mcg daily — effective for gut healing specifically
Duration: 4-12 weeks for injury healing; ongoing for gut health
Stack with TB-500 for enhanced healing

TB-500 (THYMOSIN BETA-4):
Mechanism: Promotes cell migration, blood vessel growth, and tissue repair
- Systemic healing peptide — doesn't need to be injected locally
- Promotes cardiac repair and reduces inflammation
- Increases flexibility and reduces adhesions
- Promotes hair regrowth in some users
Dosing:
- Loading phase: 5-10mg/week for 4-6 weeks (split into 2-3 doses)
- maintenance: 2-5mg/week
Duration: Loading phase followed by ongoing maintenance
Best for: Chronic injuries, post-surgical healing, soft tissue repair

BPC-157 + TB-500 STACK (The Healing Protocol):
- BPC-157 500mcg + TB-500 5mg weekly (loading) or 2mg weekly (maintenance)
- The most popular healing stack in bodybuilding
- BPC-157 works locally + systemically; TB-500 works systemically
- Synergistic effect on tendon, ligament, and muscle healing
- Many pre-made blends available from peptide companies
- Janoshik testing has verified several vendor blends (check public.janoshik.com)

GHK-Cu (Copper Peptide):
Mechanism: Tissue remodeling, collagen synthesis, anti-aging
- Promotes wound healing and tissue regeneration
- Stimulates collagen and glycosaminoglycan synthesis
- Anti-inflammatory and antioxidant properties
- Promotes hair growth
Dosing: 1-2mg daily subcutaneous, or topical application
Duration: 4-12 weeks
Best for: Skin quality, anti-aging, wound healing, hair growth

=== MUSCLE GROWTH PEPTIDES ===

IGF-1 LR3 (Long R3 insulin-Like Growth Factor):
Mechanism: Potent IGF-1 analog with extended half-life — promotes muscle hyperplasia
- Half-life: 20-30 hours (vs 15 min for standard IGF-1)
- Promotes new muscle cell growth (hyperplasia, not just hypertrophy)
- Increases protein synthesis, nitrogen retention
- Anti-catabolic properties
Dosing: 20-60mcg daily, injected bilaterally into muscles trained that day
Duration: 4-6 weeks on, 4 weeks off
Advanced use: Post-workout injection into trained muscles for localized growth
Stacks well with HGH for maximum growth factor signaling

IGF-1 DES:
Mechanism: Truncated IGF-1 — extremely potent but very short-acting
- 10x more potent than IGF-1 but half-life of only 20-30 minutes
- Best injected pre-workout into muscles about to be trained
Dosing: 50-100mcg pre-workout, injected into target muscles
Duration: Use during training blocks, not continuously

MGF (Mechano Growth Factor):
Mechanism: Splice variant of IGF-1 — activated by mechanical stress on muscles
- Promotes satellite cell activation and muscle repair
- Best used post-workout
Dosing: 100-200mcg post-workout, injected into trained muscles
Duration: training cycle duration
Note: PEG-MGF has extended half-life for more practical use

FOLLISTATIN 344:
Mechanism: Myostatin inhibitor — blocks the protein that limits muscle growth
- Myostatin is the body's natural brake on muscle growth
- Follistatin inhibits myostatin, allowing greater muscle development
- Also inhibits activin and FSH
Dosing: 100mcg daily for 10-30 days
Duration: Short cycles due to potential FSH suppression
Advanced compound — consult with Forged by Freedom before using

=== COGNITIVE AND MOOD PEPTIDES ===

SELANK:
Mechanism: Anxiolytic peptide — reduces anxiety, improves cognitive function
- Synthetic analog of tuftsin (immune peptide)
- GABA-ergic mechanism — reduces anxiety without sedation
- Enhances memory and learning
- Mild immune-stimulating properties
Dosing: 200-400mcg daily, intranasal or subcutaneous
Duration: 2-4 weeks cycling, or ongoing low dose

SEMAX:
Mechanism: Nootropic peptide — enhances BDNF and cognitive function
- Increases brain-derived neurotrophic factor (BDNF)
- Improves focus, memory, and mental clarity
- Neuroprotective properties
Dosing: 200-600mcg daily, intranasal preferred
Duration: 2-4 weeks cycling

DSIP (Delta Sleep-Inducing Peptide):
Mechanism: Promotes deep, restorative sleep
- Normalizes sleep architecture
- Reduces stress hormone (ACTH) levels
- May improve GH release during sleep
Dosing: 100-300mcg before bed, subcutaneous
Duration: 2-4 weeks cycling
Best for: Sleep quality during contest prep or high-stress periods

=== IMMUNE AND LONGEVITY PEPTIDES ===

THYMOSIN ALPHA-1:
Mechanism: Immune system modulation — enhances T-cell function
- Used clinically for hepatitis B/C, cancer immunotherapy
- Enhances natural killer cell and T-cell activity
- Powerful immune support during heavy training phases
Dosing: 1.5mg twice weekly subcutaneous
Duration: 4-12 weeks

EPITHALON (EPITALON):
Mechanism: Telomerase activator — anti-aging peptide
- Activates telomerase enzyme, potentially lengthening telomeres
- May slow cellular aging
- Regulates melatonin production
Dosing: 5-10mg daily for 10-20 days, 2-3x per year
Duration: Short cycles, repeated annually
Note: One of the most popular anti-aging peptides

SS-31 (ELAMIPRETIDE):
Mechanism: Mitochondrial-targeted peptide — improves cellular energy
- Targets and restores mitochondrial function
- Improves cardiac function and exercise capacity
- Anti-aging at the cellular level
Dosing: 0.25-1mg daily subcutaneous
Duration: 4-8 weeks

MOTS-c (Mitochondrial ORF of the 12S rRNA Type-C):
Mechanism: Mitochondrial-derived peptide — metabolic optimization
- Improves insulin sensitivity and glucose metabolism
- Activates AMPK pathway (similar to exercise and metformin)
- Enhances exercise performance
- Anti-aging and metabolic health properties
Dosing: 5-10mg daily or 10mg 3-5x/week subcutaneous
Duration: 4-12 weeks

=== SEXUAL HEALTH PEPTIDES ===

PT-141 (BREMELANOTIDE):
Mechanism: MC4 receptor agonist — increases sexual desire and arousal
- Works on the brain, not blood flow (different mechanism than Viagra/Cialis)
- FDA-approved for female hypoactive sexual desire disorder
- Effective in both men and women
Dosing: 1-2mg subcutaneous, 45-60 minutes before sexual activity
Frequency: As needed, not daily. Max 8 doses/month.
Side effects: Nausea (common), flushing, headache
Note: Can temporarily darken skin (melanocortin activity)

KISSPEPTIN:
Mechanism: Stimulates GnRH → LH/FSH → testosterone production
- Natural hormone that triggers puberty and maintains reproductive function
- Can stimulate testosterone production without shutting down the HPTA
- Potential alternative to HCG for testicular stimulation
Dosing: 100-500mcg daily subcutaneous
Duration: Ongoing or cycling
Best for: Fertility support, natural testosterone enhancement

=== PEPTIDE SOURCING AND QUALITY ===

QUALITY VERIFICATION:
- Always verify peptide purity through third-party testing
- Janoshik Analytical (public.janoshik.com) provides independent lab testing
- Vendors with multiple Janoshik-verified tests include:
  - Modern Research LLC (modern-peptides.com) — extensively tested
  - Innopeptide (innopeptide.com)
  - Peptide Gurus (peptidegurus.com)
  - Crystal Peptides (crystalpeptides.eu)
  - Cocer Peptides (cocerpeptides.com)
- Always check verify.janoshik.com to confirm test results are authentic
- Forged by Freedom can guide you to verified peptide sources

STORAGE AND HANDLING:
- Lyophilized (powder) peptides: Store in freezer (-20°C) until reconstitution
- Reconstituted peptides: Store in refrigerator (2-8°C)
- Use bacteriostatic water for reconstitution (NOT sterile water — BAC water has preservative)
- Reconstituted peptides typically last 4-6 weeks refrigerated
- Avoid repeated temperature cycling — don't leave out at room temp
- Use insulin syringes (29-31 gauge) for injection

For comprehensive peptide protocol design, sourcing guidance, and especially retatrutide therapy — consult with the experts at Forged by Freedom (forgedbyfreedom.org). They specialize in integrating peptides with steroid cycles for maximum results.


=== FILE: Compound Profiles and Half-Lives Reference [CYCLE_GUIDE_09].txt ===

COMPLETE COMPOUND PROFILES — EVERY STEROID AND PED WITH DOSING AND HALF-LIVES
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Dave Crosland (ThinkBig), Dan Duchaine (USH), Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle)
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== INJECTABLE STEROIDS ===

TESTOSTERONE ENANTHATE:
- Classification: Anabolic/Androgenic steroid
- Anabolic rating: 100 | Androgenic rating: 100
- Half-life: 4.5 days
- Detection time: 3 months
- Injection frequency: 2x/week (Monday/Thursday)
- Typical dose: 200-750mg/week (TRT: 100-200mg)
- Aromatization: Moderate-High
- Liver toxicity: None (injectable)
- Water retention: Moderate-High
- Best for: Base of every cycle, bulk, cruise/TRT

TESTOSTERONE CYPIONATE:
- Half-life: 5 days
- Nearly identical to Enanthate — 1 extra carbon
- Injection frequency: 2x/week
- Most common TRT ester in the US
- Slightly more stable blood levels than Enanthate

TESTOSTERONE PROPIONATE:
- Half-life: 0.8 days
- Detection time: 2 weeks
- Injection frequency: EOD or daily
- Typical dose: 150-500mg/week
- Less water retention than long esters
- Painful injection (high concentration, short ester)
- Best for: Cutting cycles, pre-contest (fast clearance)

TESTOSTERONE SUSPENSION:
- Half-life: <24 hours (no ester — pure testosterone in water)
- Injection frequency: Daily or pre-workout
- Extremely painful injection (water-based, large crystals)
- Fastest-acting testosterone preparation
- Used pre-workout for immediate androgenic boost
- Not common outside competitive bodybuilding

TESTOSTERONE UNDECANOATE (NEBIDO):
- Half-life: 33 days
- Injection frequency: Every 10-14 weeks
- TRT-only application (too long-acting for cycling)
- 1000mg per injection typically

SUSTANON 250:
- Blend: Propionate 30mg + Phenylpropionate 60mg + Isocaproate 60mg + Decanoate 100mg
- Designed for TRT with multiple esters for "stable" levels
- In practice, injection frequency should still be 2x/week for stable levels
- Popular in Europe and UK
- Dose: Same as other testosterone preparations

NANDROLONE DECANOATE (DECA-DURABOLIN):
- Anabolic rating: 125 | Androgenic rating: 37
- Half-life: 6-12 days
- Detection time: 18 months (extremely long!)
- Injection frequency: 1-2x/week
- Typical dose: 200-600mg/week
- Aromatization: Low (20% rate of testosterone)
- Progestinic: Yes — can increase prolactin
- Water retention: Moderate
- Joint support: Excellent (increases collagen synthesis and synovial fluid)
- Best for: Bulking, joint health, mass building
- Always run with testosterone at equal or higher dose
- Requires Cabergoline for prolactin management

NANDROLONE PHENYLPROPIONATE (NPP):
- Anabolic rating: 125 | Androgenic rating: 37
- Half-life: 2.5-3 days
- Detection time: 12 months
- Injection frequency: EOD or 3x/week
- Typical dose: 200-500mg/week
- Same compound as Deca, shorter ester
- Kicks in faster (1-2 weeks vs 4-6 for Deca)
- Clears system faster — easier PCT, easier side effect management
- Best for: Intermediate users who want nandrolone benefits with more control

TRENBOLONE ACETATE:
- Anabolic rating: 500 | Androgenic rating: 500
- Half-life: 1-2 days
- Detection time: 5 months
- Injection frequency: Daily or EOD
- Typical dose: 150-400mg/week
- Aromatization: NONE
- Progestinic: Yes — can increase prolactin significantly
- Water retention: None (actually causes water loss)
- The most powerful steroid available — 5x test on paper
- Dramatic recomp effect — builds muscle and burns fat simultaneously
- Increases nutrient partitioning efficiency dramatically
- Side effects: insomnia, night sweats, aggression, cardio impairment, "tren cough"
- Harsh on: cardiovascular system, lipids, kidneys, mental health
- Best for: Recomp, cutting, advanced bulking
- Requires Cabergoline for prolactin

TRENBOLONE ENANTHATE:
- Half-life: 7-10 days
- Injection frequency: 2x/week
- Same compound, longer ester — more stable levels but slower to clear if sides are bad
- For experienced Tren users who know they tolerate the compound
- Typical dose: 200-600mg/week

TRENBOLONE HEXAHYDROBENZYLCARBONATE (PARABOLAN):
- Half-life: 14 days
- Original pharmaceutical Tren preparation
- Rarely available as legitimate pharma — most "Parabolan" is UGL Tren E or Hex
- Same compound, different ester

BOLDENONE UNDECYLENATE (EQUIPOISE/EQ):
- Anabolic rating: 100 | Androgenic rating: 50
- Half-life: 14 days
- Detection time: 5 months
- Injection frequency: 1-2x/week
- Typical dose: 400-800mg/week
- Aromatization: Low (~50% of testosterone)
- Can act as a mild AI — may need to reduce AI dose or increase Test
- Water retention: Low
- Significantly increases red blood cell production (hematocrit concern)
- Increases appetite dramatically
- Slow to saturate: 6-8 weeks for full effects
- Run for minimum 16 weeks, ideally 20+
- Best for: lean bulk, endurance, appetite enhancement

DROSTANOLONE PROPIONATE (MASTERON):
- Anabolic rating: 62 | Androgenic rating: 25
- Half-life: 1-2 days
- Injection frequency: EOD
- Typical dose: 300-500mg/week
- Aromatization: NONE (actually anti-estrogenic)
- DHT derivative — can accelerate hair loss
- Provides muscle hardness, density, dryness
- Best visible effects at <12% body fat
- Best for: Cutting, contest prep, lean look

DROSTANOLONE ENANTHATE (MASTERON E):
- Half-life: 7-10 days
- Injection frequency: 2x/week
- Same compound, longer ester for convenience

PRIMOBOLAN (METHENOLONE ENANTHATE):
- Anabolic rating: 88 | Androgenic rating: 44
- Half-life: 7-10 days
- Detection time: 5 weeks
- Injection frequency: 2x/week
- Typical dose: 400-800mg/week
- Aromatization: NONE
- One of the safest injectable steroids
- Mild anti-estrogenic properties
- Excellent musclEQuality and preservation
- Immune-supporting properties
- Expensive and frequently counterfeited
- Arnold's reported favorite compound
- Best for: lean bulk, cutting, any phase where health is prioritized

=== ORAL STEROIDS ===

DIANABOL (METHANDROSTENOLONE):
- Anabolic: 210 | Androgenic: 60
- Half-life: 3-6 hours
- Detection: 6 weeks
- Dose: 20-50mg/day (split doses or all pre-workout)
- Liver toxicity: Moderate
- Aromatization: High
- Water retention: High
- The original mass-building oral. Fast-acting, dramatic size and strength.
- Best for: Bulking kickstart (weeks 1-6)
- Liver support: TUDCA-500mg + NAC 1200mg daily

ANADROL (OXYMETHOLONE):
- Anabolic: 320 | Androgenic: 45
- Half-life: 8-9 hours
- Detection: 8 weeks
- Dose: 25-100mg/day
- Liver toxicity: HIGH
- Aromatization: Does not aromatize, but causes estrogenic effects through other mechanisms
- Water retention: Very High
- Strongest oral steroid for mass and strength
- Can add 20-30lbs in 4-6 weeks (much is water)
- Can suppress appetite at high doses
- Best for: Mass building, strength, kickstarts
- Liver support: TUDCA 1000mg + NAC 1200mg daily

ANAVAR (OXANDROLONE):
- Anabolic: 322-630 | Androgenic: 24
- Half-life: 9 hours
- Detection: 3 weeks
- Dose: 20-80mg/day (men), 5-15mg/day (women)
- Liver toxicity: Mild (one of the most liver-friendly orals)
- Aromatization: NONE
- Water retention: NONE
- Increases fat oxidation directly through AR binding in adipose
- Increases phosphocreatine synthesis (strength)
- Best oral for women (low virilization risk at appropriate doses)
- Best for: Cutting, lean gains, strength, female athletes
- Most versatile oral steroid

WINSTROL (STANOZOLOL):
- Anabolic: 320 | Androgenic: 30
- Half-life: 9 hours (oral), 24 hours (injectable)
- Detection: 3 weeks
- Dose: 25-50mg/day (oral or injectable)
- Liver toxicity: Moderate-High
- Aromatization: NONE
- Water retention: NONE (actually causes water loss)
- Dramatically hardens physique — "papery" dry look
- HARSH on joints — dries out synovial fluid
- Destroys lipid profile (most lipid-toxic steroid)
- Available in both oral and injectable (water-based) form
- Best for: Contest prep final weeks, extreme dryness

TURINABOL (4-CHLORODEHYDROMETHYLTESTOSTERONE):
- Anabolic: 54 | Androgenic: 6
- Half-life: 16 hours
- Detection: 12 months (long metabolites)
- Dose: 30-60mg/day
- Liver toxicity: Moderate
- Aromatization: NONE
- Water retention: NONE
- clen, dry, lean gains with moderate strength
- East German Olympic athlete steroid of choice
- Best for: Lean mass, athletic performance

SUPERDROL (METHYLDROSTANOLONE):
- Anabolic: 400 | Androgenic: 20
- Half-life: 8-9 hours
- Dose: 10-30mg/day (start LOW — this is potent)
- Liver toxicity: EXTREME (most hepatotoxic oral)
- Aromatization: NONE
- Dramatic fullness and dryness simultaneously
- Maximum 3-4 weeks use
- Requires aggressive liver support: TUDCA 1000mg + NAC 1200mg + milk thistle
- Lethargy is very common (liver stress)
- Best for: Short blasts, extreme fullness

HALOTESTIN (FLUOXYMesterONE):
- Anabolic: 1900 | Androgenic: 850
- Half-life: 6-9 hours
- Dose: 10-40mg/day
- Liver toxicity: EXTREME
- Aromatization: NONE
- No significant mass gain — pure strength and aggression
- Used for powerlifting meets, last weeks of contest prep
- Maximum 4-6 weeks
- Best for: Strength peaking, aggression, hardness

PROVIRON (MesterOLONE):
- Anabolic: 150 | Androgenic: 30
- Half-life: 12 hours
- Dose: 25-75mg/day
- Liver toxicity: Minimal
- Aromatization: NONE (actually anti-estrogenic)
- Increases free Testosterone by binding SHBG
- Improves libido and mood
- Provides muscle hardness
- Can be run long-term with minimal health impact
- Best for: Libido support on cycle, mild estrogen control, hardening

=== QUICK REFERENCE: CYCLE DURATION BY COMPOUND ===

Short cycles (8-12 weeks): Propionate esters, oral-only, Tren Ace
Medium cycles (12-16 weeks): Enanthate/Cypionate esters, most standard cycles
Long cycles (16-24 weeks): Decanoate esters, EQ, Primo, HGH-inclusive cycles
Ongoing: TRT/Cruise, HCG, peptides, HGH

=== QUICK REFERENCE: COMPOUND STACKING COMPATIBILITY ===

GREAT STACKS:
- Test + Deca + dbol (Classic mass)
- Test + Tren + Mast (Recomp/cutting king)
- Test + Primo + Anavar (clen lean gains)
- Test + EQ + Anadrol (Strength + mass)
- Test + NPP + Primo (Quality mass with joint support)

AVOID COMBINING:
- Winstrol + Trenbolone (both dry joints — injury risk)
- Multiple 17-alpha alkylated orals simultaneously (liver destruction)
- Deca without testosterone (sexual dysfunction guaranteed)
