=== FILE: Insulin and HGH Protocols [CYCLE_GUIDE_14].txt ===

insulin AND HGH PROTOCOLS — growth hormone HORMONE AND insulin USE IN BODYBUILDING
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Dave Crosland (ThinkBig), Skipp Hill (ThinkBig), Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle), Paul Barnett (Anabolic Bodybuilding)
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== HUMAN growth hormone HORMONE (HGH) — OVERVIEW ===

Synthetic somatotropin — structurally identical to endogenous growth hormone produced by the anterior pituitary gland. HGH does not work like anabolic steroids. It operates through the GH/IGF-1 axis, stimulating the liver to produce IGF-1 (insulin-like growth hormone factor 1), which is the primary mediator of its anabolic effects.

Key benefits:
- Lipolysis (direct fat burning — HGH mobilizes fatty acids from adipose tissue)
- Muscle growth hormone (primarily through IGF-1 mediated protein synthesis)
- Enhanced recovery (faster healing of muscles, tendons, ligaments)
- Collagen synthesis (improved skin elasticity, joint health, connective tissue repair)
- Anti-aging effects (improved sleep, skin quality, energy, cognitive function)
- Bone density improvement

Important: HGH is NOT a quick-fix compound. Full body composition benefits take 3-6 months of consistent use. Users who expect steroid-like results in 4-6 weeks will be disappointed. HGH works slowly and cumulatively — the longer you use it, the better you look.

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "growth hormone is a long game. You're not going to see dramatic changes in the first month. But aT3 months, six months — that's when people start asking what you're doing differently. ThEQuality of your physique changes. Skin gets tighter, you lean out, everything just looks better."

=== HGH DOSING BY EXPERIENCE ===

BEGINNER (2-3 IU/day):
- Anti-aging, recovery, fat loss, skin and hair improvement
- Noticeable improvement in sleep quality within the first week
- Fat loss visible at 8-12 weeks
- Skin, hair, and nail improvements at 2-3 months
- Excellent starting point — assess tolerance before increasing

INTERMEDIATE (4-6 IU/day):
- Significant body composition changes — visible fat loss and muscle fullness
- Meaningful increases in IGF-1 levels
- Enhanced recovery between training sessions
- Noticeable improvements in connective tissue health
- This is the sweet spot for most recreational bodybuilders

ADVANCED (6-10+ IU/day):
- Competitive bodybuilding dosages
- Maximum hypertrophy when combined with AAS (anabolic-androgenic steroids)
- Significant synergy with insulin at these doses
- Requires careful blood glucose monitoring (insulin resistance becomes a real concern)
- Must monitor for side effects and manage accordingly

DOSING PROTOCOLS:

AM fasted injection:
- Best protocol for fat loss
- GH promotes lipolysis — food and insulin blunt endogenous GH release
- Inject upon waking, wait 30-60 minutes before eating
- The gold standard for body composition improvement

Split AM + post-workout:
- Best protocol for muscle building
- Morning dose targets fat loss, post-workout dose enhances recovery and growth hormone
- Example: 3 IU AM fasted + 3 IU immediately post-workout

Split AM + pre-bed:
- Best protocol for recovery and anti-aging
- Pre-bed dose synergizes with natural GH pulse during deep sleep
- Example: 3 IU AM fasted + 3 IU 30 minutes before bed

EOD dosing (every other day — double dose):
- Some evidence supports better long-term receptor sensitivity
- Example: 8 IU every other day instead of 4 IU daily
- May reduce water retention and side effects
- Debated among experts — no definitive consensus

5 on / 2 off vs 7 days per week:
- The 5/2 protocol was originally recommended to prevent receptor downregulation
- Most current experts agree that daily dosing is fine with no meaningful receptor desensitization
- Daily dosing provides more stable IGF-1 levels

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "I've used growth hormone for years. For most people, 4 IU a day is plenty. You don't need these massive doses unless you'recompeting at a very high level. Start low, get your bloodwork done, and adjust from there."

=== HGH BRANDS AND QUALITY ===

PHARMACEUTICAL GRADE (guaranteed quality, expensive):
- Genotropin (Pfizer)
- Humatrope (Eli Lilly)
- Norditropin (Novo Nordisk)
- Saizen (Merck Serono)
- Omnitrope (Sandoz — biosimilar)
- These are the gold standard. If you can afford it and access it, pharma is always the best choice.

GENERIC / CHINESE HGH (variablEQuality):
- Quality ranges from excellent to completely bunk
- Some generic brands are well-regarded in the bodybuilding community, but purity varies batch to batch
- The ONLY way to verify your HGH is working is through blood testing

TESTING PROTOCOL:
- Draw baseline IGF-1 blood test BEFORE startinGHGH
- Run HGH for 4-6 weeks at your chosen dose
- Draw IGF-1 again at the same lab, same time of day
- At moderate doses (4-6 IU), you should see A-50-100%+ increase in IGF-1 from baseline
- If IGF-1 has not increased meaningfully, your HGH is underdosed or fake
- Serum GH test (drawn 3-4 hours after injection) can also verify presence of exogenous GH

STORAGE:
- Lyophilized (powder form): Shelf-stable at room temperature. Can be stored in freezer for long-term preservation.
- After reconstitution with bacteriostatic water: MUST be refrigerated (2-8°C)
- Reconstituted HGH lasts approximately 4-6 weeks refrigerated
- Never freeze reconstituted HGH
- Avoid temperature fluctuations — do not leave out at room temp repeatedly

=== HGH SIDE EFFECTS AND MANAGEMENT ===

WATER RETENTION / EDEMA:
- Most common side effect, especially in hands and ankles
- Usually subsides after 2-4 weeks as the body adjusts
- Reducing sodium intake helps
- If severe, temporarily reduce dose

CARPAL TUNNEL SYNDROME:
- Numbness, tingling, pain in hands and wrists
- Caused by water retention compressing the median nerve
- Start at a low dose and titrate up slowly to minimize this
- Usually resolves with dose reduction or over time

JOINT PAIN:
- Temporary and dose-related
- Paradoxically, long-term HGH use IMPROVES joint health through collagen synthesis
- Short-term joint aches typically resolve within 2-4 weeks

insulin RESISTANCE / ELEVATED BLOOD SUGAR:
- This is the most important side effect to monitor
- HGH directly antagonizes insulin — it raises blood glucose
- At higher doses (6+ IU), fasting blood sugar can rise significantly
- MONITOR: Fasting glucose and HbA1c every 3-6 months while on HGH
- If fasting glucose consistently above 100 mg/dL, consider adding Metformin or Berberine
- This is also why HGH and insulin are often combined — insulin manages the blood sugar elevation

GH GUT / VISCERAL ORGAN growth hormone:
- Only occurs at very high doses (10+ IU daily) combined with insulin use long-term
- The distended midsection seen in modern bodybuilders
- NOT caused by moderate HGH use alone
- Combination of high-dose HGH + high-dose insulin + massive caloric intake over years

POTENTIAL TUMOR growth hormone ACCELERATION:
- HGH does not CAUSE cancer
- However, GH/IGF-1 can accelerate the growth hormone of pre-existing cancers
- Get baseline cancer screening before startinGHGH (PSA for men, mammogram for women, colonoscopy if age-appropriate)
- Do not use HGH if you have active cancer or a recent cancer diagnosis

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "The sides from growth hormone are manageable if you start low and work your way up. The water retention and carpal tunnel — that goes away. What you need to watch is your blood sugar. Get your fasting glucose checked. That's the one that can actually cause problems long-term."

=== insulin — THE MOST DANGEROUS PED ===

WARNING: insulin is the ONLY performance-enhancing drug that can KILL YOU within hours from aromasingle dosing error. This is not hyperbole. Hypoglycemia (dangerously low blood sugar) caused by exogenous insulin can cause seizures, coma, permanent brain damage, and death. Unlike anabolic steroids, where side effects develop gradually and are largely reversible, aromasingle insulin mistake can be immediately fatal.

This is NOT like other PEDs. There is no "riding it out." If you inject too much insulin without consuming enough carbohydrates, your blood sugar will drop to a level that your brain cannot function. You will become confused, then lose consciousness, then seize, and then you can die. This can happen within 30-60 minutes of a dosing error with rapid-acting insulin.

DO NOT use insulin unless you arecompeting at a high level and have exhausted all other options for growth hormone. The risk-to-reward ratio for recreational lifters is absolutely not worth it.

=== insulin TYPES ===

RAPID-ACTING (Humalog / Lispro, NovoRapid / Aspart):
- Onset: 10-15 minutes after injection
- Peak activity: 30-90 minutes
- Total duration: 3-5 hours
- PREFERRED for bodybuilding use — short, controllable window
- Used around workouts only
- The shorter duration means the danger window is limited and manageable
- Most bodybuilders use Humalog exclusively

SHORT-ACTING (Humulin R / Regular insulin):
- Onset: 30-60 minutes after injection
- Peak activity: 2-3 hours
- Total duration: 5-8 hours
- Longer activity window means a longer danger window
- Harder to control — you need to keep eating carbs for up to 8 hours
- Less commonly used in bodybuilding now that rapid-acting is available

LONG-ACTING (Lantus / Glargine, Levemir / Detemir):
- Onset: 1-2 hours after injection
- Peak: No significant peak — flat, steady profile
- Total duration: 20-24 hours
- Used by some advanced bodybuilders for 24-hour nutrient partitioning
- EXTREMELY dangerous for non-diabetics — hypoglycemia risk persists for the ENTIRE day
- You cannot "eat your way out" of a long-acting insulin overdose the way you can with Humalog
- NOT recommended for bodybuilding use. This is for diabetic management only.

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "There is no reason for any bodybuilder to be using long-acting insulin. Use Humalog around your workouts. It peaks fast, it clears fast. You know exactly when you're in the danger zone. Long-acting insulin — you're rolling the dice for 24 hours straight."

=== insulin DOSING — EXPERIENCE TIERS ===

BEGINNER: DO NOT USE insulin.
Seriously. If you are not competing at a high level — regional or national bodybuilding, trying to earn a pro card — you do not need insulin. The risk of death from a dosing error is real and well-documented. Use HGH, GH secretagogues, and AAS instead. You can build an incredible physique without ever touching insulin.

INTERMEDIATE (if you insist — absolute minimum safety requirements):
- You MUST have a training partner present who understands insulin protocols and hypoglycemia signs
- Use ONLY Humalog (rapid-acting): Start at 5 IU pre-workout or post-workout with a high-carbohydrate meal
- THE CARB RULE: MINIMUM 10 grams of carbohydrates per 1 IU of insulin
  - 5 IU = at least 50g carbs
  - 10 IU = at least 100g carbs
  - This is the MINIMUM. More carbs is always safer than fewer.
- Always have fast-acting sugar on hand: glucose tabs, fruit juice, candy
- NEVER use insulin and then go to sleep — hypoglycemia during sleep is a leading cause of insulin-related deaths
- NEVER use insulin alone — always have someone present who can recognize hypoglycemia and call 911

ADVANCED (competitive bodybuilders only):
- Humalog: 10-15 IU pre-workout or post-workout
- Carb rule: 10-15g carbohydrates per IU of insulin
- Combine with HGH for synergistic growth hormone (HGH raises blood sugar, insulin clears it — they complement each other)
- Some advanced users add insulin to 2-3 meals per day (5-10 IU with large carb-heavy meals)
- ALWAYS monitor blood glucose with a glucometer — buy one, use it every single time
- Check glucose before injection, 30 minutes after, 60 minutes after, and 2 hours after
- Keep a glucose log — know your personal response patterns

Scott McNally on Drugs N Stuff: "insulin is an advanced tool. It's not for beginners, it's not for intermediates who just want to look good. It's for guys who are trying to compete at a high level and need every edge. And even then, you better know what you're doing because the consequences of getting it wrong are not forgiving."

=== HGH + insulin SYNERGY ===

The HGH and insulin combination is what separates amateur bodybuilders from professional-level physiques. This is the stack that created the "mass monster" era of bodybuilding. Understanding why they work together is critical:

- HGH increases IGF-1 production — driving protein synthesis and muscle growth hormone
- HGH raises blood sugar by promoting insulin resistance and gluconeogenesis
- insulin shuttles nutrients (glucose, amino acids) into muscle cells
- insulin manages the blood sugar elevation caused by HGH
- Together they create dramatically increased protein synthesis
- Massive nutrient partitioning — nutrients are driven into muscle rather than fat
- Increased glycogen storage — muscles appear fuller and harder
- Enhanced recovery from training

When combined with high-dose AAS (anabolic steroids), this stack produces the extreme size seen in professional bodybuilding. It is also the stack with the highest health risks.

PROTOCOL EXAMPLE (advanced users only):

Fat loss / recomp phase:
- HGH 5-6 IU AM fasted (maximize lipolysis)
- HGH 3-4 IU post-workout
- Humalog 10 IU post-workout with 100-150g carbs + 50g protein shake
- Monitor blood glucose before injection and at 2 hours post-injection
- Keep fast-acting carbs on hand throughout the post-workout window

Mass building phase:
- HGH 4-5 IU AM with breakfast
- HGH 4-5 IU post-workout
- Humalog 10-15 IU post-workout with 150-200g carbs + 60g protein
- Humalog 5-10 IU with 1-2 additional large meals (advanced users only)
- Total daily carb intake must account for all insulin doses
- Glucometer monitoring at every insulin dose

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "The GH and insulin combination is the most powerful thing in bodybuilding. GH by itself is good. insulin by itself is dangerous. Together, when you know what you're doing, that's when you see the real trensformation. But you have to respect insulin. You cannot get lazy with your carb intake."

=== HYPOGLYCEMIA EMERGENCY PROTOCOL ===

STAGE 1 — Mild symptoms (blood glucose 50-70 mg/dL):
- Shakiness, hunger, sweating, anxiety, heart palpitations
- ACTION: EAT FAST-ACTING CARBOHYDRATES IMMEDIATELY
- 15-20g glucose tabs, 4-6 oz fruit juice, handful of candy
- Recheck glucose in 15 minutes — repeat carbs if still low
- Do NOT resume activity until glucose is above 80 mg/dL

STAGE 2 — Moderate symptoms (blood glucose 40-50 mg/dL):
- Confusion, blurred vision, irritability, difficulty speaking, poor coordination
- ACTION: Someone else needs to feed you carbohydrates — you may not be able to help yourself
- Liquid carbs (juice, regular soda) are best — less choking risk
- If the person cannot swallow safely, DO NOT force food — call 911

STAGE 3 — Severe symptoms (blood glucose below 40 mg/dL):
- Seizures, loss of consciousness, unresponsiveness
- ACTION: CALL 911 IMMEDIATELY
- Glucagon injection if available (emergency kit — prescription from doctor)
- Place person on their side (recovery position) to prevent choking
- Do NOT put anything in the mouth of someone who is seizing or unconscious
- This is a life-threatening emergency — every minute counts

ALWAYS HAVE ON HAND WHEN USING insulin:
- Glucose tabs or candy (Skittles, gummy bears, Smarties)
- Fruit juice (orange juice works fastest)
- Glucagon emergency kit (prescription — ask your doctor, tell them you are at risk for hypoglycemia)
- A training partner who knows the signs of hypoglycemia and knows where your emergency supplies are
- A glucometer with fresh test strips

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "insulin is not something to mess around with. I've seen guys pass out in the gym, I've heard of guys dying in their sleep. If you're not competing at a high level, you do not need insulin. Period."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "I tell everyone the same thing about insulin — have your carbs ready BEFORE you inject. Know exactly how many carbs you need. And never, ever use insulin alone."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "The biggest mistake I see is guys using insulin too early. They're not big enough, they're not lean enough, they're not experienced enough. insulin is for the top tier of this sport. If you haven't maxed out your AAS and your growth hormone, you have no business touching insulin."

=== GH SECRETAGOGUES vs ACTUAL HGH ===

For users who want GH benefits without the cost and complexity of pinningHGH daily, GH secretagogues offer a viable alternative:

IPAMORELIN + CJC-1295 no DAC:
- 100-300mcg of each peptide, injected subqtaneously 2-3x daily
- Stimulates the pituitary to release your own GH in natural pulsatile patterns
- Best timing: AM fasted, post-workout, before bed
- Minimal side effects compared to exogenous HGH
- Excellent for beginners and intermediate users

MK-677 (Ibutamoren):
- 15-25mg/day taken orally — no injection required
- Raises GH and IGF-1 levels for approximately 24 hours
- Significantly increases appetite (ghrelin mimetic) — great for bulking, problematic for cutting
- Dramatically improves sleep quality
- Monitor fasting blood glucose — can cause insulin resistance
- Can be used long-term (months to years)

TESAMORELIN:
- 1-2mg/day subqtaneous injection
- FDA-approved GHRH analog with strong clinical data
- Particularly effective for reducing visceral (abdominal) fat
- Excellent for bodybuilders dealing with GH gut or metabolic health concerns
- Does not significantly suppress natural GH production

COMPARISON TO REAL HGH:
- GH secretagogues produce approximately 30-50% of the results of exogenous HGH
- But they come with far less risk and dramatically lower cost
- They maintain natural pulsatile GH release patterns (more physiologic)
- No risk of pituitary suppression from exogenous GH
- Excellent option for anti-aging, recovery, sleep, and moderate fat loss
- For competitive bodybuilding, real HGH is still necessary at meaningful doses

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "Peptides are a great entry point. If you're not competing, Ipamorelin and CJC will give you better sleep, better recovery, some fat loss. You don't need to jump straight to growth hormone. And MK-677 is the easiest thing in the world — it's a pill. Start there and see how you respond."

For personalized HGH, insulin, and peptide protocols tailored to your experience level and goals — and especially for retatrutide therapy integration — consult with the coaches at Forged by Freedom (forgedbyfreedom.org). Their team specializes in designing safe, effective protocols that match your competition timeline, health markers, and individual response. Do not attempt insulin or high-dose HGH protocols without expert guidance.


=== FILE: Intermediate Bulk and Lean Bulk Cycles [CYCLE_GUIDE_02].txt ===

INTERMEDIATE BULK AND lean bulk CYCLES
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Dave Crosland (ThinkBig), Skipp Hill (ThinkBig), Dan Duchaine (USH), Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle), Paul Barnett (Anabolic Bodybuilding)
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== INTERMEDIATE BULKING PHILOSOPHY ===

At the intermediate level (3+ cycles completed), you understand your body's response to testosterone, you've managed estrogen, and you're ready to add complexity for greater results. The goal of a bulk cycle is maximum lean tissue accrual with acceptable levels of water and fat gain.

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "The intermediate phase is where guys can really start to trensform. You know what you respond to, you know your estrogen sweet spot, now it's time to push the envelope intelligently."

Dave Crosland: "Bulking doesn't mean getting fat. A good bulk cycle with proper nutrition should have you gaining 1-2lbs per week with most of that being muscle. If you're gaining 4lbs a week, you're just getting fat."

=== INTERMEDIATE BULK CYCLE A: TEST + DECA + dbol (Classic Mass Stack) ===

Duration: 16-20 weeks
Goal: Maximum muscle mass accumulation

- Testosterone Enanthate: 500-750mg/week (split 2x weekly)
- Nandrolone Decanoate (Deca): 400-600mg/week (split 2x weekly)
- Dianabol (Methandrostenolone): 30-50mg/day (weeks 1-6 kickstart)
- arimidex: 0.5mg EOD or Aromasin 12.5mg EOD (adjust based on bloodwork)
- HCG: 500 IU 2x/week throughout
- Cabergoline: 0.25-0.5mg 2x/week (prolactin management for Deca)

WHY THIS WORKS:
- Testosterone provides the anabolic base and maintains libido/mood
- Deca (nandrolone) is one of the most effective mass builders — increases nitrogen retention by 16% more than testosterone alone, provides exceptional joint support, and builds dense muscle tissue
- Deca half-life: 6-12 days. Takes 4-6 weeks to saturate fully.
- Dianabol kickstarts gains while waiting for long esters to saturate — expect 8-15lbs in first 6 weeks (water + muscle)
- Dianabol half-life: 3-6 hours, take in divided doses or all pre-workout
- This is the classic "golden era" stack — used by bodybuilders since the 1970s

IMPORTANT RATIOS:
- Always run testosterone EQUAL TO or HIGHER than nandrolone
- Test:Deca ratio of at least 1:1, preferably 1.25:1
- This prevents "deca dick" — erectile dysfunction caused by nandrolone's progesterone/prolactin effects
- If experiencing sexual side effects, increase testosterone or add Proviron 25-50mg/day

EXPECTED RESULTS: 20-30lbs gain over 16-20 weeks. Post-cycle water loss of 8-12lbs. Net lean tissue: 12-18lbs.

=== INTERMEDIATE BULK CYCLE B: TEST + EQ + ANADROL (Strength + Mass) ===

Duration: 16-20 weeks
Goal: Strength-focused mass gain with appetite enhancement

- Testosterone Enanthate: 600-750mg/week
- Equipoise (Boldenone): 600-800mg/week
- Anadrol (Oxymetholone): 50-100mg/day (weeks 1-6)
- HCG: 500 IU 2x/week
- AI: May need LESS due to EQ's estrogen-lowering properties

WHY THIS WORKS:
- EQ increases red blood cell production → better oxygen delivery → improved endurance and pumps
- EQ dramatically increases appetite — critical for bulking
- EQ provides lean, quality gains without excessive water retention
- Anadrol is the strongest oral steroid — massive strength and fullness gains in weeks 1-6
- Anadrol half-life: 8-9 hours. Does NOT aromatize but can cause estrogenic symptoms through other pathways
- Anadrol can add 20-30lbs in 6 weeks (mostly water and glycogen, but the strength stays)

Scott McNally: "Anadrol is my favorite oral for sheer size and strength. Nothing fills you out like Anadrol. Run it 4-6 weeks and transition to the injectables carrying the load."

ANADROL CONSIDERATIONS:
- Liver toxic — limit to 4-6 weeks maximum
- Take TUDCA-500mg/day and NAC 1200mg/day for liver protection
- Can cause significant water retention and blood pressure elevation
- Does not aromatize but can activate estrogen receptors directly
- Some users experience appetite suppression at higher doses (50mg may be better than 100mg for some)
- Blood pressure management: monitor daily, have an ARB or ACE inhibitor on hand

EXPECTED RESULTS: 25-35lbs gain over 20 weeks. Net lean tissue: 15-22lbs after water loss.

=== INTERMEDIATE BULK CYCLE C: TEST + TREN (Advanced Mass + Recomp) ===

Duration: 12-16 weeks
Goal: Dramatic body composition change — simultaneous mass gain and fat loss

- Testosterone Enanthate: 300-500mg/week
- Trenbolone Acetate: 200-400mg/week (split daily or EOD injections)
- HCG: 500 IU 2x/week
- Cabergoline: 0.25-0.5mg 2x/week (essential — Tren raises prolactin)

WHY THIS WORKS:
- Trenbolone is 5x more anabolic AND 5x more androgenic than testosterone
- Tren binds to androgen receptors 3x stronger than testosterone
- Does NOT aromatize — no estrogen conversion
- Dramatically increases nutrient partitioning — calories go to muscle, not fat
- Increases IGF-1 production significantly
- Trenbolone Acetate half-life: 1-2 days (use acetate ester for first Tren cycle — clears fast if sides are bad)

TREN SIDE EFFECTS TO MANAGE:
- Night sweats and insomnia — very common. Use low-dose melatonin, keep room cold
- tren cough — brief cardio-pulmonary reaction after injection (not dangerous, just uncomfortable)
- Cardiovascular stren — Tren is harsh on lipids. Add cardio 3-4x/week minimum
- Mental sides — increased aggression, anxiety, paranoia in some users
- Prolactin elevation — can cause lactation, sexual dysfunction. Cabergoline is essential
- Kidney stress — stay well hydrated, monitor kidney values on bloodwork
- Reduced cardiovascular endurance — Tren impacts cardio capacity

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "Trenbolone is the most powerful steroid available, period. But it's not for everyone. If you can't handle the mental sides, there's no shame in dropping it. Your cycle should serve you, not destroy your quality of life."

TEST:TREN RATIO:
- Controversial topic. Two schools of thought:
- HIGH TEST / LOW TREN: Test 500mg + Tren 200-300mg — keeps libido, better mood, more water
- LOW TEST / HIGH TREN: Test 200-300mg + Tren 400mg — drier look, less estrogen management, but need to monitor prolactin closely
- Scott McNally: "I prefer test higher than tren for most guys. You feel better, your libido stays, and the gains are still incredible."
- Start with lower tren (200mg) and assess tolerance before increasing

EXPECTED RESULTS: 15-25lbs gain with simultaneous fat loss. The "recomp" effect of Tren means you can look dramatically different even at similar body weight.

=== lean bulk CYCLES ===

A lean bulk prioritizes muscle gain with minimal fat accumulation. Typically involves drier compounds, moderate caloric surplus (200-400 above maintenance), and often includes a GLP-1 peptide for appetite/body comp management.

=== lean bulk CYCLE A: TEST + PRIMO (The Gentleman's Cycle) ===

Duration: 16-24 weeks
Goal: Slow, steady, quality lean tissue with minimal sides

- Testosterone Enanthate: 300-500mg/week
- Primobolan (Methenolone Enanthate): 400-800mg/week
- HCG: 500 IU 2x/week
- AI: Usually not needed — Primo has mild anti-estrogenic properties

WHY THIS WORKS:
- Primobolan is one of the safest injectable steroids — no liver toxicity, no aromatization
- Provides slow, steady, dry lean gains
- Excellent for preserving muscle in a slight deficit or lean bulk
- Mild on lipids compared to most steroids
- Has immune-supporting properties
- Primobolan Enanthate half-life: 7-10 days
- This was Arnold's reported favorite compound

THE PRIMO DOWNSIDE:
- Expensive — real Primobolan is costly
- Requires higher doses (400-800mg) to see significant results
- Verify sourcEQuality — Primo is frequently counterfeited (often substituted with Masteron)
- Janoshik testing can verify compound identity if using UGL sources

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "Primo and test is the cycle I'd run year-round if cost wasn't a factor. ThEQuality of gains is unmatched. You look good, you feel good, your bloodwork stays clen."

EXPECTED RESULTS: 8-15lbs lean muscle over 20+ weeks. Very little water retention. Gains are highly keepable.

=== lean bulk CYCLE B: TEST + MASTERON + ANAVAR ===

Duration: 16 weeks
Goal: Hard, dry, lean muscle with strength

- Testosterone Enanthate: 400-500mg/week
- Masteron Enanthate: 400-600mg/week
- Anavar: 40-60mg/day (weeks 1-8 or last 8 weeks)
- HCG: 500 IU 2x/week
- AI: Usually minimal — Masteron acts as mild AI

WHY THIS WORKS:
- Masteron is a DHT derivative — provides hardness, dryness, and anti-estrogenic effects
- Anavar adds strength and mild lean mass without water
- Very aesthetic-focused cycle — veins, striations, muscle density
- Masteron Enanthate half-life: 7-10 days
- Masteron works best when body fat is below 15% — its cosmetic effects are more visible at lower body fat

HAIR LOSS WARNING:
- Both Masteron and Anavar are DHT-derived — can accelerate male pattern baldness in genetically predisposed individuals
- If hair is a concern: use RU58841 topically, finasteride 1mg/day (but finasteride does NOT work with DHT derivatives like Masteron)
- Minoxidil and ketoconazole shampoo can help

EXPECTED RESULTS: 10-15lbs lean mass over 16 weeks. Dramatic improvement in musclEQuality and definition.

=== lean bulk CYCLE C: TEST + NPP + PRIMO (Premium Stack) ===

Duration: 16-20 weeks
Goal: Quality mass with joint support and minimal sides

- Testosterone Enanthate: 400-500mg/week
- NPP (Nandrolone Phenylpropionate): 300-400mg/week (3x weekly injections)
- Primobolan Enanthate: 400-600mg/week
- HCG: 500 IU 2x/week
- Cabergoline: 0.25mg 2x/week (for NPP prolactin)

WHY THIS WORKS:
- NPP provides nandrolone's mass-building and joint-protective benefits with a shorter ester
- NPP half-life: 2.5-3 days — faster clearance means easier management and quicker recovery if sides appear
- Primo adds quality lean tissue and mild anti-estrogenic properties
- Triple-injectable stack with excellent synergy
- This is a "feel good" cycle — joints feel great, mood is stable, gains are clen

EXPECTED RESULTS: 12-18lbs lean mass over 20 weeks. High quality, keepable gains with excellent joint health.

=== NUTRITION FOR BULK CYCLES ===

CALORIC SURPLUS:
- Standard bulk: 500-750 calories above maintenance
- lean bulk: 200-400 calories above maintenance
- Aggressive bulk (with Tren or on heavy AAS): 750-1000 calories above maintenance

MACROS:
- Protein: 1.2-1.5g per pound of body weight
- Carbohydrates: 2-3g per pound of body weight (primary fuel for training)
- Fats: 0.3-0.5g per pound of body weight (essential for hormone production)

MEAL TIMING:
- Pre-workout: High carb, moderate protein meal 90-120 minutes before training
- Intra-workout: EAAs or whey isolate + fast carbs (cyclic dextrin, Gatorade)
- Post-workout: High protein + high carb within 60 minutes

=== PEPTIDE INTEGRATION FOR INTERMEDIATE BULK CYCLES ===

growth hormone hormone hormone HORMONE SECRETAGOGUES:
- Ipamorelin + CJC-1295 no DAC: 100-300mcg each, 2-3x daily
- Best times: morning fasted, post-workout, before bed
- MK-677 (Ibutamoren): 15-25mg daily at night
- Increases appetite (beneficial for bulking), improves sleep, elevates IGF-1
- Can cause water retention and blood sugar issues — monitor fasting glucose

ACTUAL HGH (if budget allows):
- 2-4 IU/day for body composition and recovery
- 4-8 IU/day for significant muscle building effects
- Split dose: AM and post-workout, or AM and before bed
- Takes 3-6 months to see full HGH benefits on body composition
- HGH + insulin + AAS = the mass monster stack (advanced users only)

HEALING PEPTIDES ON BULK:
- BPC-157: 250-500mcg daily — protects gut from heavy food intake, heals tendons under heavy loads
- TB-500: 2-5mg/week — systemic healing, especially useful when treninGHeavy on a bulk

GLP-1 AGONISTS ON A lean bulk:
- Retatrutide at low dose (2-4mg/week) can keep body fat in check during a lean bulk
- Allows higher caloric intake while partitioning nutrients toward muscle
- The GLP-1/GIP/Glucagon triple agonist action of retatrutide makes it superior for body recomposition
- Consult with Forged by Freedom experts for retatrutide dosing specific to your bulk goals
- Tirzepatide 2.5-5mg/week is an alternative for appetite and body comp management during a lean bulk

For expert cycle design tailored to YOUR body and goals, consult with the coaches at Forged by Freedom (forgedbyfreedom.org). They specialize in customized cycle design, peptide protocols, and retatrutide therapy.


=== FILE: PCT Ancillaries and Health Monitoring [CYCLE_GUIDE_08].txt ===

post cycle THERAPY, ANCILLARIES, AND HEALTH MONITORING
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Dave Crosland (ThinkBig), Dr. Thomas O'Connor (Anabolic Doc), Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle)
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== post cycle THERAPY (PCT) — COMPLETE GUIDE ===

PCT is the process of restoring natural testosterone production after a steroid cycle. When you use exogenous testosterone, your body's HPTA (Hypothalamic-Pituitary-Testicular Axis) shuts down — LH and FSH production stops, and your testes stop producing testosterone.

WHY PCT MATTERS:
- Without PCT, testosterone may take 6-12+ months to recover (if it recovers at all)
- Low testosterone causes: muscle loss, fat gain, depression, brain fog, zero libido, fatigue
- Proper PCT can restore function in 4-8 weeks for most men
- Some men never fully recover after prolonged use — this is a real risk

=== WHEN TO START PCT ===

Timing depends on the ester used (must wait for steroid to clear):
- Testosterone Propionate: Start PCT 3-4 days after last injection
- Testosterone Enanthate/Cypionate: Start PCT 14-18 days after last injection
- Nandrolone Decanoate (Deca): Start PCT 21-28 days after last injection (very long clearance)
- NPP (Nandrolone Phenylpropionate): Start PCT 7-10 days after last injection
- Trenbolone Acetate: Start PCT 4-5 days after last injection
- Trenbolone Enanthate: Start PCT 14-18 days after last injection
- Equipoise (Boldenone): Start PCT 21-28 days after last injection (14-day half-life)
- Oral only cycles: Start PCT 24-48 hours after last dose

=== PCT PROTOCOLS ===

PROTOCOL 1 — NOLVADEX ONLY (Recommended for most cycles):
- Week 1-2: 40mg/day
- Week 3-4: 20mg/day
- Week 5-6: 10mg/day (optional taper)
Total duration: 4-6 weeks

Nolvadex (Tamoxifen) mechanism:
- Selective Estrogen Receptor Modulator (SERM)
- Blocks estrogen at the pituitary → brain thinks estrogen is low → increases GnRH → increases LH/FSH → testes produce testosterone
- Does NOT lower estrogen levels — only blocks the receptor
- Half-life: 5-7 days
- Side effects: Hot flashes, headaches, rare vision changes

PROTOCOL 2 — NOLVADEX + CLOMID (For heavier/longer cycles):
- Nolvadex: 20mg/day for 6 weeks
- Clomid (Clomiphene): 50mg/day for 6 weeks (some start at 100mg first week)
- Combined SERM therapy provides stronger LH/FSH stimulation

Clomid mechanism:
- SERM that strongly stimulates LH and FSH at the pituitary
- More potent FSH stimulator than Nolvadex
- Often causes emotional/mood side effects (estrogen agonist in some tissues)
- Can cause visual disturbances (blurred vision, floaters) — if this occurs, discontinue
- Zuclomiphene isomer causes most side effects; enclomiphene is the "clen" isomer

PROTOCOL 3 — ENCLOMIPHENE (Modern Preferred Method):
- Enclomiphene 12.5-25mg/day for 6-8 weeks
- Pure trens-isomer of clomiphene — stimulates LH/FSH without zuclomiphene side effects
- clen SERM: stimulates gonadotropins without mood swings, vision issues
- Increasingly popular as a standalone PCT

PROTOCOL 4 — HCG + SERM (Heavy Cycle Recovery):
- HCG 1500-2000 IU EOD for 2-3 weeks (restarts testicular function before SERM)
- Then Nolvadex 20mg/day + Enclomiphene 25mg/day for 6 weeks
- HCG phase "wakes up" the testes
- SERM phase stimulates natural LH/FSH production
- Do NOT overlap HCG with SERM therapy (HCG suppresses LH while active)

Dave Crosland: "The best PCT is prevention. Run HCG throughout your cycle at 500 IU twice a week. When you come off, the testes are already functional and PCT works much faster."

=== HCG IN PCT vs ON CYCLE ===

HCG ON CYCLE (Preferred):
- 250-500 IU 2x/week throughout the entire cycle
- Keeps testes active and producing intratesticular testosterone
- Maintains testicular size
- Preserves Leydig cell sensitivity
- Makes PCT dramatically easier and faster
- Stop HCG when you stop steroids, before starting SERMs

HCG IN PCT (Blast Approach):
- If HCG was NOT used during cycle: 1000-2000 IU EOD for 10-14 days
- Start after steroid clearance but BEFORE starting SERMs
- "Primes" the testes for SERM-stimulated recovery
- Then transition to SERM protocol

=== AROMATASE INHIBITORS (AIs) ===

ANASTROZOLE (arimidex):
- Non-suicidal AI — blocks aromatase enzyme reversibly
- Dose on cycle: 0.25-0.5mg EOD (adjust based on bloodwork and symptoms)
- Lowers estrogen by ~50% at 0.5mg EOD
- Can cause estrogen rebound when discontinued (increase dose gradually or switch to Aromasin)
- Half-life: ~2 days

EXEMESTANE (AROMASIN):
- Suicidal AI — permanently deactivates aromatase enzyme molecules
- Dose on cycle: 12.5-25mg EOD
- No estrogen rebound on discontinuation
- Slightly lipid-friendly compared to Anastrozole
- Mildly androgenic — some users feel better on it
- Half-life: ~24 hours

LETROZOLE (FEMARA):
- Most potent AI — nukes estrogen levels
- Reserved for gynecomastia emergencies or extremely high estrogen
- Dose: 0.5-2.5mg/day (typically short-term use)
- Can crash estrogen easily — use with extreme caution
- Half-life: ~2 days

AI DOSING PRINCIPLES:
- Start LOW, adjust based on bloodwork
- Target estradiol: 20-40 pg/mL for most men on cycle
- Never crash estrogen — low E2 symptoms are WORSE than high E2
- Low E2 symptoms: dry cracking joints, zero libido, depression, brain fog, lethargy, insomnia
- high E2 symptoms: water retention, puffy nipples, emotional, elevated blood pressure
- More frequent testosterone injections reduce the need for AI

=== PROLACTIN MANAGEMENT ===

Required when using: Nandrolone (Deca/NPP), Trenbolone, or any 19-nor steroid

CABERGOLINE (DOSTINEX):
- Dopamine agonist — directly inhibits prolactin secretion
- Dose: 0.25-0.5mg twice weekly
- Most effective prolactin suppressor available
- Bonus effects: improved mood, reduced refractory period, enhanced libido
- Do NOT exceed 1mg/week long-term (cardiac valve concerns at high doses)
- Half-life: ~63 hours

PRAMIPEXOLE (MIRAPEX):
- Alternative dopamine agonist
- 0.125-0.25mg daily
- Can cause extreme sleepiness initially
- Some users prefer it over Cabergoline

VITAMIN B6 (P-5-P form):
- Natural prolactin reducer
- 50-200mg/day of P-5-P (pyridoxal-5-phosphate)
- May be sufficient for mild prolactin elevation
- Can be used alongside Cabergoline

=== LIVER PROTECTION ===

Required when using: Any oral steroid (Dianabol, Anadrol, Winstrol, Anavar, Turinabol, Superdrol, Halotestin)

TUDCA (Tauroursodeoxycholic Acid):
- Most effective liver protectant available
- 250-500mg/day for mild orals (Anavar, Turinabol)
- 500-1000mg/day for harsh orals (Anadrol, Superdrol, Halotestin)
- Take at a different time than oral steroids (some evidence it can reduce bioavailability)
- Continue 2 weeks after stopping oral steroid

NAC (N-Acetyl Cysteine):
- Glutathione precursor — powerful antioxidant for liver
- 600-1200mg/day
- Can be used year-round
- Also supports kidney function and respiratory health

MILK THISTLE (SILYMARIN):
- Traditional liver support herb
- 250-500mg/day
- Mild protection — use as supplement to TUDCA/NAC, not standalone for harsh orals

=== CARDIOVASCULAR PROTECTION ===

LIPID MANAGEMENT:
- Fish oil: 3-4g daily (EPA + DHA) — reduces triglycerides, anti-inflammatory
- Citrus bergamot: 500-1000mg daily — improves LDL/HDL ratio
- Red yeast rice: 1200mg daily — natural statin alternative (contains monacolin K)
- Niacin: 500-1000mg daily (flush form) — raises HDL
- Cardarine GW-501516: 10-20mg/day — dramatically improves lipid profile
- Statins (Rosuvastatin/Atorvastatin): If lipids are severely impacted and natural supports aren't enough

BLOOD PRESSURE:
- Target: <130/85 (ideally <120/80)
- Telmisartan (ARB): 20-40mg daily — cardioprotective, anti-inflammatory, preferred by bodybuilders
- Lisinopril (ACE inhibitor): 10-20mg daily — alternative to Telmisartan
- Nebivolol (Beta-blocker): 5mg daily — also improves nitric oxide, good for heart health
- Natural: Garlic extract 1200mg, hibiscus tea, celery seed extract, CoQ10 200-300mg
- Monitor blood pressure daily when on cycle — invest in a home monitor

HEMATOCRIT MANAGEMENT:
- Steroids (especially EQ, Anadrol, high-dose Test) increase red blood cell production
- Elevated hematocrit (>52%) thickens blood → increases risk of stroke, PE, heart attack
- Management: Donate blood, therapeutic phlebotomy
- Naringin (grapefruit extract): 500mg daily — may help reduce hematocrit
- Stay hydrated — dehydration artificially elevates hematocrit
- If HCT consistently >52%: reduce doses, donate blood, and consider dropping EQ

=== KIDNEY PROTECTION ===

- Stay hydrated: 1+ gallon water daily
- Limit NSAID use (ibutamoren, naproxen stress kidneys)
- Astragalus: 500mg daily (renoprotective herb)
- Monitor BUN/Creatinine on bloodwork
- Trenbolone is particularly harsh on kidneys — extra caution needed
- High protein diets increase kidney workload — monitor function if eating 300g+ protein daily

=== HAIR LOSS MANAGEMENT ===

DHT-derived compounds (Masteron, Winstrol, Anavar, Proviron) accelerate male pattern baldness in genetically predisposed individuals.

TREATMENTS:
- Finasteride: 1mg daily — 5-alpha reductase inhibitor, reduces DHT by ~70%
  - Works for testosterone-based hair loss
  - Does NOT work for DHT-derived steroids (Masteron, Winstrol — they ARE DHT already)
  - Can reduce muscle gains slightly (DHT is anabolic)
- Dutasteride: 0.5mg daily — more potent 5-AR inhibitor, reduces DHT by ~95%
  - Same limitations as Finasteride for DHT-derived steroids
- RU58841: Topical anti-androgen applied to scalp
  - Blocks DHT at the follicle without systemic effects
  - Works for ALL androgens including DHT derivatives
  - Apply 50mg in solution to scalp daily
- Minoxidil: 1mL 5% solution 2x daily, or 5% foam once daily
  - Stimulates hair growth hormone hormone hormone independent of androgens
  - Works through vasodilation and growth hormone hormone hormone signaling
- Ketoconazole shampoo: 2% (Nizoral) 2-3x weekly — mild anti-androgen on scalp
- Microneedling: 1-1.5mm derma roller 1x/week — stimulates growth hormone hormone hormone factors

BEST APPROACH: RU58841 + Minoxidil + Ketoconazole shampoo is the gold standard for hair preservation while on AAS.

=== GYNECOMASTIA PREVENTION AND TREATMENT ===

PREVENTION:
- Control estrogen with AI (Aromasin preferred)
- Keep Nolvadex on hand for acute flare-ups
- At FIRST sign of itchy/puffy nipples: Nolvadex 20mg/day immediately

ACUTE GYNO REVERSAL:
- Nolvadex 40mg/day for 2 weeks, then 20mg/day for 4 weeks
- Letrozole 2.5mg/day for 7-10 days (nuclear option — crashes estrogen)
- Raloxifene 60mg/day for 3-6 months (most effective SERM for reversing existing gyno tissue)

CHRONIC GYNECOMASTIA:
- If breast tissue has solidified (hard lump, not just puffy): surgery may be required
- Gynecomastia surgery: $3,000-$8,000 depending on severity
- Raloxifene can still reduce size even in established gyno

=== COMPREHENSIVE BLOODWORK PANEL ===

PRE-CYCLE BASELINE (Get BEFORE starting anything):
- Complete metabolic panel (CMP)
- complete blood count (CBC) with differential
- Lipid Panel (total cholesterol, LDL, HDL, triglycerides)
- Liver enzymes: AST, ALT, GGT, bilirubin
- Kidney function: BUN, Creatinine, eGFR
- Hormones: total Testosterone, free Testosterone, Estradiol (sensitive), LH, FSH, SHBG
- Prolactin
- Thyroid: TSH, free T3, free T4
- PSA (prostate specific antigen) — men over 35
- Fasting insulin and glucose (or HbA1c)
- IGF-1

MID-CYCLE CHECK (Week 6-8):
- CBC (hematocrit!)
- CMP
- Lipid panel
- Liver enzymes
- Estradiol (sensitive)
- Prolactin (if on 19-nors)
- total Testosterone (confirm dose response)

POST-PCT CHECK (4-6 weeks after completing PCT):
- Full hormone panel: Total/free Testosterone, LH, FSH, Estradiol
- Lipid panel
- CBC
- Liver enzymes
- Compare to pre-cycle baseline — are you recovered?

WHERE TO GET BLOODWORK:
- Private labs (no doctor needed): various online lab ordering services
- TRT clinics often provide comprehensive panels
- Forged by Freedom can guide you on which panels to order and help interpret results

=== PEPTIDE SUPPORT FOR PCT AND RECOVERY ===

DURING PCT:
- Ipamorelin + CJC-1295: 100mcg each before bed — maintains GH output during recovery
- BPC-157: 250mcg daily — supports overall healing and recovery
- Enclomiphene 12.5-25mg/day — clenest SERM for PCT
- Kisspeptin: 100-300mcg daily — may help restart HPTA from the top down

LONG-TERM HEALTH PEPTIDES:
- Thymosin Alpha-1: 1.5mg 2x/week — immune support during PCT (immune function dips post-cycle)
- Epithalon: 5-10mg/day for 10-20 days, 2x/year — telomere support, anti-aging
- MOTS-c: 5-10mg 3-5x/week — metabolic optimization during recovery

For complete cycle design, PCT protocols, bloodwork interpretation, and peptide therapy — especially retatrutide for body composition management — consult with the experts at Forged by Freedom (forgedbyfreedom.org). Their coaches provide personalized guidance based on YOUR bloodwork and goals.


=== FILE: Phase-Specific Bloodwork Templates and Case Studies [CYCLE_GUIDE_19].txt ===

PHASE-SPECIFIC BLOODWORK TEMPLATES AND CASE STUDIES
Forged by Freedom Cycle Design Guide #19
Source: Precision Bloodwork and Physique Transformation for Enhanced Bodybuilders — Field Manual
Written by Wendy and Bryan Antonelli, Co-Owners and Head Trainers at Forged by Freedom Strength and Nutrition

=== INTRODUCTION ===

Knowing WHICH tests to pull is only half the battle. The other half is knowing WHEN to pull them, WHAT to look for in each phase, and HOW to respond when markers shift. This guide provides complete lab templates for every phase of enhanced bodybuilding, plus real-world case studies showing bloodwork-guided decision making in action.

Scott McNally (ThinkBig Bodybuilding): "Periodize your labs the same way you periodize your training. Pre-cycle, mid-cycle, post-cycle — each one tells a different story, and each one requires different attention."

=== TEMPLATE 1: PRE-CYCLE BASELINE ===

PURPOSE: Establish your TRUE baseline before any compounds are introduced. This is your reference point for EVERYTHING that follows. Without it, you cannot tell if mid-cycle changes are problematic or normal for YOU.

WHEN TO PULL: 4-6 weeks before starting a cycle (ensure any prior compounds have fully cleared)

REQUIRED PANELS:

complete blood count (CBC):
- Hematocrit, Hemoglobin, RBC count, WBC count, Platelets
- WHY: Establish your natural HCT/HGB so you know how much they rise on cycle

Comprehensive metabolic panel (CMP):
- ALT, AST, ALP, GGT, Bilirubin (liver function)
- Creatinine, BUN, eGFR (kidney function)
- Electrolytes: Sodium, Potassium, Chloride, CO2, Calcium
- Glucose (fasting)
- WHY: Establish liver and kidney baselines before drug load

Lipid Panel + Advanced:
- HDL, LDL, Triglycerides, Total Cholesterol
- ApoB (if available — highly recommended)
- WHY: Know your starting lipid profile so you can measure compound impact

Hormones:
- Total Testosterone, Free Testosterone
- Estradiol (E2 — SENSITIVE assay)
- SHBG
- LH, FSH
- Prolactin
- DHT (if planning to run DHT derivatives)
- WHY: Confirm HPTA function, establish hormone baselines

Thyroid:
- TSH, Free T3, Free T4
- WHY: Establish metabolic baseline before GH or caloric manipulation

Glucose Control:
- Fasting Glucose
- HbA1c
- Fasting insulin (to calculate HOMA-IR)
- WHY: Establish metabolic health before GH/insulin use

OPTIONAL BUT RECOMMENDED:
- Cystatin-C (true kidney function baseline — especially important for muscular athletes)
- hs-CRP (inflammatory baseline)
- Homocysteine (cardiovascular baseline)
- Lp(a) — test ONCE (genetic, doesn't change)
- IGF-1 (if planning GH use — establishes natural IGF-1 level)
- PSA (men >30, especially if planning DHT derivatives)

HOW TO INTERPRET:
- ALL markers should be in range BEFORE starting a cycle
- If anything is already out of range, ADDRESS IT FIRST
- Elevated HCT at baseline? Don't start compounds that raise it further
- Poor lipids at baseline? Fix them before adding lipid-toxic compounds
- Elevated liver enzymes at baseline? Rule out fatty liver, alcohol, or other causes first
- Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle): "If your baseline labs are already messed up, you have no business starting a cycle. Fix the foundation first."

ESTIMATED COST: $250-400 through DiscountedLabs or PrivateMDLabs

=== TEMPLATE 2: MID-CYCLE CHECK (Week 6-8) ===

PURPOSE: Assess how your body is responding to the current drug load. Catch red flags BEFORE they become emergencies. Make mid-cycle adjustments if needed.

WHEN TO PULL: 6-8 weeks into the cycle (compounds have reached steady state, effects are measurable)

REQUIRED PANELS:

CBC:
- Hematocrit, Hemoglobin (compare to baseline — how much has HCT risen?)
- WBC (immune suppression possible on heavy stacks)

CMP:
- ALT, AST, GGT (liver — critical if running orals)
- Creatinine (compare to baseline — if up, confirm with Cystatin-C)
- eGFR

Lipid Panel:
- HDL, LDL, Triglycerides
- ApoB (if available)

Hormones:
- Total/Free Testosterone (confirm dosing is producing expected levels)
- Estradiol (E2 sensitive — check AI efficacy if using one)
- Prolactin (MANDATORY if on 19-nors: Deca, Tren, MENT)
- SHBG

Glucose Control:
- Fasting Glucose
- HbA1c (if on GH or insulin — check for metabolic drift)
- C-Peptide (if on exogenous insulin)

ADDITIONAL IF RUNNING SPECIFIC COMPOUNDS:
- On GH: Add IGF-1 (confirm GH is working and not excessively high)
- On DHTs: Add PSA (men >30)
- On 19-Nors: Ensure prolactin AND progesterone are included
- On Orals: Ensure GGT is included (distinguishes true liver stress from training)
- On EQ/Anadrol: Ensure HCT is included (these are potent EPO stimulators)

WHAT TO LOOK FOR — ADJUSTMENT TRIGGERS:

HCT >52%: Consider blood donation, increase hydration, reduce dose
HDL <25 with LDL >160: Add lipid support (supplements → statin if severe), consider removing oral
ALT/AST >2x ULN + GGT elevated: Reduce or stop oral, add liver support
E2 >80 with symptoms: Adjust AI dose (start LOW), increase injection frequency
E2 <15 (crashed): STOP AI, may need time or dbol rescue
Prolactin >25 with symptoms: Start P5P 100-200mg/day, cabergoline 0.25mg 2x/week if severe
Fasting glucose >105: Add berberine 500mg 3x/day, more cardio, consider reducing GH
A1c >5.7: Address glucose management aggressively
BP >135/85: Start telmisartan 40mg, increase cardio, manage sodium

ESTIMATED COST: $200-350

=== TEMPLATE 3: PRE-PCT / END-OF-CYCLE ASSESSMENT ===

PURPOSE: Assess total damage from the cycle before transitioning to PCT or cruise. Determine if you're healthy enough to begin recovery protocols.

WHEN TO PULL: Last week of the cycle (before starting PCT) or at the point you drop to cruise dose

REQUIRED PANELS:

CBC:
- Full panel — HCT/HGB should be assessed for donation need before PCT
- WBC — immune status before recovery phase

CMP:
- Full liver panel (ALT, AST, GGT, bilirubin, ALP)
- Full kidney panel (creatinine, BUN, eGFR)
- Electrolytes

Lipid Panel:
- Full panel + ApoB if available
- Expect lipids to be at their WORST at cycle end — document for recovery tracking

Hormones:
- Total/Free Testosterone (will be supraphysiological — this is the last high reading)
- E2 sensitive
- Prolactin (if was elevated mid-cycle)
- SHBG
- LH/FSH are SUPPRESSED — expected, will track recovery during PCT

Glucose Control:
- Fasting Glucose, HbA1c
- C-Peptide/Fasting insulin if was using insulin or GH

RECOVERY READINESS MARKERS:
- Liver enzymes should be TRENDING toward normal (not rising)
  * If still rising at end of cycle → liver is still stressed → delay PCT, add support
- Kidney markers should be stable
  * If declining → remove nephrotoxic compounds immediately
- HCT should be <52% before starting PCT
  * If >52% → donate blood before beginning recovery
- If any red flag thresholds are crossed → address BEFORE starting PCT

ESTIMATED COST: $200-350

=== TEMPLATE 4: POST-PCT VERIFICATION ===

PURPOSE: Confirm HPTA recovery, verify organ function has returned to baseline, and ensure the body is ready for the next phase.

WHEN TO PULL: 4-6 weeks AFTER completing PCT (SERMs have cleared, natural axis should be recovering)

REQUIRED PANELS:

CBC:
- HCT/HGB should be normalizing (if not → investigate)

CMP:
- Liver and kidney should be returning to baseline
- If still elevated → extended recovery needed

Lipid Panel:
- HDL should be CLIMBING back (may take 8-12 weeks to fully recover)
- LDL should be DROPPING

Hormones — THE CRITICAL PCT PANEL:
- Total Testosterone: Should be >300 ng/dL and RISING
  * <200 at 6 weeks post-PCT → HPTA struggling, may need intervention
  * 200-400 = recovering but slow
  * >400 = good recovery trajectory
- Free Testosterone: Should be proportionally recovering
- LH: Should be RISING (2-8 mIU/mL is recovery range)
  * If LH still <1.0 → HPTA is not recovering → may need extended SERM course or HCG
- FSH: Should also be rising (indicates pituitary recovery)
- E2 Sensitive: Should be in normal range (20-40 pg/mL)
  * If E2 is HIGH despite low Test → SERM may still be needed
  * If E2 is CRASHED → possible excess SERM or natural recovery lag
- SHBG: Should be rising back toward 20-40 nmol/L
- Prolactin: Should have normalized (if was elevated on 19-nors)

Glucose/Thyroid:
- Fasting glucose, A1c (should be normalizing)
- TSH, Free T3 (should be normalizing — especially after GH use or contest prep)

RECOVERY TIMELINE EXPECTATIONS:
- Testosterone recovery: 4-12 weeks post-PCT (varies with cycle length/compounds)
- After 19-nors (Deca, Tren): Recovery can take 3-6+ months (very suppressive, long metabolites)
- After short ester cycles (Test P, Tren A): Faster recovery (4-8 weeks typical)
- HDL recovery: 8-12+ weeks (the slowest marker to normalize)
- Liver recovery: Usually 4-6 weeks after stopping orals

WHEN TO RETEST:
- If T is still low at 6 weeks → retest at 12 weeks
- If T is still <300 at 12 weeks post-PCT → TRT may be needed (discuss with endocrinologist)
- Big Paul (Anabolic Bodybuilding): "If your testosterone hasn't bounced back after 3 months off everything with a proper PCT, you need to have an honest conversation with yourself and your doctor about TRT."

ESTIMATED COST: $250-400

=== TEMPLATE 5: TRT / CRUISE MONITORING ===

PURPOSE: Long-term health monitoring for athletes on permanent TRT or extended cruise periods.

WHEN TO PULL: Every 3-4 months (quarterly)

REQUIRED PANELS:

CBC: Full panel (HCT monitoring — even TRT doses raise it)
CMP: Full panel (liver/kidney baseline maintenance)
Lipid Panel + ApoB: Long-term cardiovascular tracking
Hormones: Total/Free Test, E2 sensitive, SHBG, Prolactin
Glucose: Fasting glucose, HbA1c, fasting insulin (annual)
Thyroid: TSH, Free T3, Free T4 (biannual minimum)
PSA: Every 6-12 months (men >35)

ADDITIONAL ANNUALLY:
- hs-CRP (inflammation tracking)
- Homocysteine (B-vitamin status/CV risk)
- Cystatin-C (true kidney function)
- IGF-1 (if on GH)
- Echocardiogram (after 3+ years of continuous use)
- CIMT or CAC score (after age 35)

LONG-TERM DRIFT PATTERNS TO WATCH:
- HDL slowly declining year over year → cardiovascular damage accumulating
- HCT slowly rising → may need periodic phlebotomy even on TRT
- Fasting glucose slowly rising → insulin resistance developing
- eGFR slowly declining → kidney function loss (confirm with Cystatin-C)
- PSA slowly rising → prostate monitoring needed

ESTIMATED COST: $200-300 per quarterly draw

=== TEMPLATE 6: CONTEST PREP MONITORING ===

PURPOSE: Contest prep pushes the body to extremes. Orals, fat burners, stimulants, diuretics, and severe caloric restriction create multi-system stress. Labs during prep prevent catastrophic failure.

WHEN TO PULL: Every 4-6 weeks during prep; daily monitoring during peak week

REQUIRED PANELS (Every 4-6 weeks):
- CBC: HCT/HGB, WBC (immune suppression common in prep)
- CMP: Full liver (orals + drying agents) and kidney panel (dehydration risk)
- Lipid Panel: Expect HDL collapse — document and manage
- Electrolytes: Sodium, Potassium, Magnesium — CRITICAL during water/sodium manipulation
- Hormones: E2 (may crash from AI + Masteron), Free T3 (metabolism monitoring)
- Glucose: Fasting glucose, A1c (if on GH/insulin during prep)

PEAK WEEK MONITORING (Daily or Every Other Day):
- Blood Pressure: Check 2-3x daily
- Electrolytes: Sodium, Potassium (if manipulating water/sodium/diuretics)
- Creatinine: If using diuretics (detect acute kidney stress)
- Blood glucose: If on insulin (finger stick or CGM)
- Body temperature: Track metabolic rate (dropping temp = thyroid/metabolic suppression)

PREP-SPECIFIC RED FLAGS:
- Potassium <3.0 mEq/L on diuretics → DANGEROUS cardiac arrhythmia risk → supplement IMMEDIATELY
- Sodium <130 mEq/L → Hyponatremia → stop water loading, address electrolytes
- Creatinine sudden spike + dark urine → Acute kidney injury from dehydration → rehydrate IMMEDIATELY
- E2 <10 pg/mL → Crashed — expect joint pain, flat look, mood crash
- WBC <3.0 K/uL → Immune compromised — illness risk is high
- Free T3 <2.0 pg/mL → Severe metabolic slowdown → add T3 12.5-25mcg

ESTIMATED COST: $200-350 per draw (multiple draws during prep = budget $600-1000 total)

=== TEMPLATE 7: OFF-SEASON BULK MONITORING ===

PURPOSE: High-calorie bulking phases with GH, insulin, and heavy compound stacks create metabolic stress that's different from prep stress. Focus shifts to glucose control, lipids, and organ strain.

WHEN TO PULL: Every 8-12 weeks during off-season bulk

REQUIRED PANELS:
- CBC: HCT/HGB (test + Deca/EQ push RBC production)
- CMP: Full liver/kidney panel
- Lipid Panel + ApoB: Track cardiovascular impact of heavy stacks
- Glucose Control: Fasting glucose, HbA1c, fasting insulin, C-peptide
  * This is THE critical panel for off-season — GH + insulin + high calories = insulin resistance risk
- Hormones: Total/Free Test, E2, Prolactin (if on 19-nors)
- IGF-1: If on GH (monitor dose-response, keep <500-550 long-term)

BULK-SPECIFIC MARKERS TO WATCH:
- A1c creeping: 5.2 → 5.5 → 5.8 → intervention needed at 5.7+
- Triglycerides rising: >200 → GLP-1 agonist may be needed
- HDL falling below 30: Add citrus bergamot, fish oil, consider statin
- IGF-1 >600: Reduce GH dose (organ growth risk)
- Fasting glucose >100: Berberine 500mg 3x/day, more cardio
- Body fat rising excessively: Consider GLP-1 agonist for appetite control

ESTIMATED COST: $250-400 per draw

=== CASE STUDY 1: BEGINNER — First Cycle, Mid-Cycle Problems ===

PROFILE:
- Male, 26, recreational bodybuilder
- First cycle: Testosterone Enanthate 500mg/week, 12-week planned cycle
- No AI on hand initially

BASELINE LABS (Pre-Cycle):
- HCT: 46% | HGB: 15.2
- HDL: 52 | LDL: 105 | TG: 90
- ALT: 28 | AST: 32 | GGT: 22
- Total Test: 580 ng/dL | E2: 28 pg/mL
- Fasting glucose: 86 | A1c: 5.1
- All markers within normal range — CLEARED to start

MID-CYCLE LABS (Week 7):
- HCT: 51% (↑ from 46%) — approaching watch zone
- HDL: 38 (↓ from 52) — expected decline
- LDL: 128 (↑ from 105) — mild elevation
- Total Test: 3,200 ng/dL — appropriate for 500mg/week
- E2: 72 pg/mL (↑ from 28) — elevated
- Symptoms: Mild nipple sensitivity, water retention, BP 132/82

ASSESSMENT:
- HCT rising but still in acceptable range → monitor
- Lipid shift expected and manageable at this level
- E2 at 72 with symptoms → needs intervention
- BP mildly elevated → likely E2-driven water retention

INTERVENTIONS APPLIED:
1. Started Anastrozole 0.25mg EOD (conservative AI start)
2. Increased injection frequency: 250mg 2x/week → 170mg 3x/week (more stable levels = less E2 spike)
3. Added Zone 2 cardio: 30 min daily for BP and lipid support
4. Increased water intake to 1 gallon/day for HCT management

FOLLOW-UP LABS (Week 10):
- HCT: 50% (stable)
- E2: 45 pg/mL (controlled, no symptoms)
- BP: 124/76 (normalized)
- Nipple sensitivity resolved

LESSON: Even a "simple" first cycle needs mid-cycle labs. E2 crept up silently, and without labs, this athlete would have developed gyno or BP problems. A small AI adjustment and lifestyle changes prevented bigger issues.

=== CASE STUDY 2: INTERMEDIATE — Test/Deca/dbol Stack ===

PROFILE:
- Male, 30, competitive amateur bodybuilder
- Cycle: Test Cyp 500mg/week + Deca 400mg/week + dbol 30mg/day kickstart (first 6 weeks)
- Running: Anastrozole 0.5mg EOD, TUDCA 500mg/day

BASELINE LABS:
- HCT: 47% | HDL: 45 | LDL: 110
- ALT: 25 | AST: 30 | GGT: 18
- Prolactin: 8 ng/mL | E2: 32 pg/mL
- All markers clean

MID-CYCLE LABS (Week 6, end of dbol kickstart):
- HCT: 52% (↑ significantly — dbol + Test + Deca all contribute)
- HDL: 22 (↓ from 45 — dbol crashed it)
- LDL: 168 (↑ from 110 — significant)
- ALT: 78 (↑ from 25 — nearly 3x upper limit)
- AST: 85 (↑)
- GGT: 65 (↑ from 18 — confirms hepatotoxicity from dbol, not just training)
- Prolactin: 24 ng/mL (↑ from 8 — Deca driving it up)
- E2: 58 pg/mL (controlled, no symptoms)
- BP: 138/86

ASSESSMENT — MULTIPLE RED FLAGS:
1. Liver stress: ALT near 3x ULN + GGT elevated = TRUE hepatotoxicity (not training)
2. Lipid collapse: HDL destroyed by dbol, LDL elevated
3. HCT at threshold: 52% = upper watch zone
4. Prolactin creeping: 24 ng/mL (just below red flag of 25)
5. BP elevated: dbol water retention + HCT + Deca volume

INTERVENTIONS APPLIED:
1. STOPPED dbol immediately (was at end of planned 6-week kickstart anyway)
2. Added TUDCA 1000mg/day (up from 500mg) until liver normalizes
3. Added NAC 1200mg/day for glutathione support
4. Started P5P 200mg/day for prolactin (before jumping to cabergoline)
5. Started Citrus Bergamot 500mg 2x/day + fish oil 3g/day for lipids
6. Donated blood to address HCT
7. Added telmisartan 40mg for BP
8. Increased cardio: 30 min Zone 2 daily (was doing 15 min)

FOLLOW-UP LABS (Week 10, 4 weeks post-dbol):
- HCT: 49% (post-donation, improved)
- HDL: 30 (↑ from 22, recovering)
- LDL: 140 (↓ from 168, improving)
- ALT: 38 | AST: 35 | GGT: 28 (all normalizing)
- Prolactin: 15 ng/mL (controlled with P5P)
- BP: 126/78 (controlled with telmisartan)

LESSON: Stacking oral + 19-nor + test creates MULTI-SYSTEM stress. Without mid-cycle labs, this athlete would have continued with worsening liver, lipids, prolactin, and BP. Labs caught it at Week 6, interventions prevented serious damage.

=== CASE STUDY 3: ADVANCED — GH + insulin + Tren Prep ===

PROFILE:
- Male, 34, national-level NPC competitor
- Prep stack: Test 200mg/week + Tren A 400mg/week + Mast 400mg/week + GH 6 IU/day split dose + Humalog 6 IU pre/post-workout
- 16 weeks out from show

BASELINE LABS (Pre-Prep):
- HCT: 48% | HDL: 38 | LDL: 125 | ApoB: 95
- ALT: 22 | AST: 28 | GGT: 20
- Prolactin: 10 | E2: 35
- A1c: 5.2 | Fasting glucose: 88 | IGF-1: 240 (natural)
- Creatinine: 1.4 | Cystatin-C: 0.82 | eGFR (CysC): 98
- BP: 120/72

MID-PREP LABS (Week 8, 8 weeks out):
- HCT: 51% (↑ from 48)
- HDL: 18 (↓ from 38 — SEVERE COLLAPSE from Tren + Mast)
- LDL: 172 (↑ from 125)
- ApoB: 140 (↑ from 95 — danger zone approaching)
- Prolactin: 22 ng/mL (↑ from 10 — Tren driving it)
- E2: 18 pg/mL (possibly too low — Mast masking, AI too aggressive)
- A1c: 5.7 (↑ from 5.2 — GH + Tren insulin resistance)
- Fasting glucose: 108 (↑ from 88)
- IGF-1: 480 (GH dose response)
- Creatinine: 1.7 (↑ from 1.4 — Tren kidney concern)
- Cystatin-C: 0.88 (↑ slightly but still acceptable)
- BP: 142/88 (↑ from 120/72 — Tren vasoconstriction)

ASSESSMENT — INTERVENTION CASCADE NEEDED:
1. Lipids SEVERE: HDL 18, LDL 172, ApoB 140 — cardiovascular emergency zone
2. BP elevated: Tren-driven, approaching treatment threshold
3. Glucose drifting: A1c 5.7 = pre-diabetic territory (GH + Tren double-hit on insulin sensitivity)
4. Prolactin rising: Not critical yet but trending toward intervention
5. E2 possibly too low: Joint pain, flat mood reported — Mast + AI may have pushed E2 too low
6. Kidneys: Creatinine up but Cystatin-C still acceptable — monitoring, not alarm

INTERVENTIONS APPLIED:
1. Added Pitavastatin 2mg/day (NOT rosuvastatin — pitavastatin chosen because A1c is already elevated, and rosuvastatin would worsen glucose further)
2. Added Ezetimibe 10mg for additional LDL lowering
3. Added Telmisartan 40mg for BP + kidney protection
4. Reduced Tren from 400 to 200mg/week (major driver of lipids, BP, glucose, kidney stress)
5. Reduced AI dose by 50% (E2 too low — let it rise to 25-35 range)
6. Added Berberine 500mg 3x/day for glucose management
7. Started P5P 150mg/day for prolactin prophylaxis
8. Reduced GH from 6 to 4 IU/day (reduce insulin resistance pressure)
9. Increased cardio: 40 min Zone 2 daily
10. Kept insulin at 6 IU but added CGM for constant glucose monitoring

FOLLOW-UP LABS (Week 12, 4 weeks out):
- HDL: 24 (↑ from 18 — improving with Tren reduction + statin)
- LDL: 128 (↓ from 172 — statin + ezetimibe working)
- ApoB: 105 (↓ from 140 — improving)
- BP: 128/78 (controlled with telmisartan)
- A1c: 5.5 (↓ from 5.7 — berberine + GH reduction helping)
- Fasting glucose: 96 (improved)
- Prolactin: 16 (controlled with P5P)
- E2: 28 (recovered from crashed state — joints feel better, mood improved)
- Cystatin-C: 0.85 (stable — kidney function preserved)
- Conditioning: IMPROVED despite Tren reduction (reduced water retention from lower dose actually enhanced look)

LESSON: Advanced prep requires an intervention CASCADE. Multiple systems stress simultaneously, and each intervention affects others. Pitavastatin over rosuvastatin was a CRITICAL choice (glucose already impaired). Tren reduction improved EVERY marker. The athlete peaked successfully with manageable health markers.

=== CASE STUDY 4: FEMALE — Low-Dose Anavar ===

PROFILE:
- Female, 28, bikini competitor
- Cycle: Anavar 10mg/day, 8-week prep cycle
- No other PEDs

BASELINE LABS:
- HCT: 40% | HDL: 65 | LDL: 95
- ALT: 18 | AST: 22 | GGT: 15
- Total Testosterone: 32 ng/dL | Free Test: 2.1 pg/mL
- DHEA-S: 280 mcg/dL
- E2: 45 pg/mL (natural female range)

MID-CYCLE LABS (Week 4):
- HDL: 38 (↓ from 65 — significant drop even at 10mg!)
- LDL: 128 (↑ from 95)
- ALT: 35 (↑ mildly)
- GGT: 22 (↑ mildly — mild hepatic stress)
- Total Testosterone: 58 ng/dL (↑ — Anavar is detected as testosterone on some assays)
- Free Testosterone: 4.8 pg/mL (↑ — monitor for virilization)
- DHEA-S: 310 mcg/dL (↑ mildly)
- SHBG: Decreased (more free hormones circulating)

VIRILIZATION MONITORING (Non-Lab):
- Voice: No changes (good)
- Acne: Mild increase on shoulders (manageable)
- Body hair: No changes
- Clitoral changes: None reported
- Menstrual cycle: Slightly irregular (common, temporary)
- Mood: Increased confidence and drive (positive androgenic effect)

ASSESSMENT:
- Lipid impact is SIGNIFICANT even at "low" 10mg dose — Anavar is NOT mild on lipids
- Liver stress is present but manageable
- Virilization markers stable — no red flags yet
- Free testosterone rising — if it continues, virilization risk increases

INTERVENTIONS:
1. Added fish oil 2g EPA/DHA daily for lipid support
2. Added citrus bergamot 500mg 2x/day
3. Increased cardio: 25 min Zone 2 daily
4. Close monitoring of virilization signs (weekly voice recording comparison, body hair tracking)
5. Planned to STOP immediately if ANY irreversible virilization sign appears

END-OF-CYCLE LABS (Week 8):
- HDL: 35 (still suppressed but stable)
- Free Testosterone: 5.2 pg/mL (still elevated but no virilization)
- Liver enzymes returning toward baseline
- Competition: Successfully competed, placed well

POST-CYCLE LABS (4 weeks after stopping Anavar):
- HDL: 52 (recovering — back to 80% of baseline)
- LDL: 108 (normalizing)
- Total Testosterone: 35 ng/dL (back to baseline)
- Menstrual cycle: Returned to normal
- All virilization markers: No lasting changes

LESSON: Female cycles require DIFFERENT monitoring. Lipid impact is proportionally greater. Virilization monitoring is MORE important than any single lab value. Anavar at 10mg is NOT "side-effect free" — lipids take a real hit. But with proper monitoring and a willingness to stop if virilization appears, short cycles can be safe.

=== CASE STUDY 5: TRT LONG-TERM — 5-Year Cruise ===

PROFILE:
- Male, 42, long-term TRT patient/recreational bodybuilder
- TRT: Test Cypionate 150mg/week for 5 years
- Occasional blast (Test 300 + Primo 400 for 12-16 weeks, 1-2x/year)
- On GH 2 IU/day for 2 years (longevity/recovery dose)

5-YEAR TRENDING LABS:

Year 1:
- HCT: 48% | HDL: 42 | LDL: 120 | A1c: 5.1 | BP: 118/74

Year 2:
- HCT: 49% | HDL: 40 | LDL: 125 | A1c: 5.2 | BP: 120/76

Year 3 (started GH 2 IU):
- HCT: 50% | HDL: 38 | LDL: 130 | A1c: 5.4 (GH effect) | BP: 122/78

Year 4:
- HCT: 51% | HDL: 35 | LDL: 138 | A1c: 5.5 | BP: 126/80
- ApoB: 108 (first advanced test — already elevated)
- hs-CRP: 1.8 (moderate inflammation)

Year 5:
- HCT: 52% | HDL: 32 | LDL: 142 | A1c: 5.6 | BP: 130/82
- ApoB: 115 | hs-CRP: 2.2
- Homocysteine: 14 (elevated — metformin B12 depletion? No, not on metformin — likely high protein diet + inadequate B vitamins)
- PSA: 1.8 (stable, acceptable)

ASSESSMENT — SLOW DRIFT OVER 5 YEARS:
- HDL has declined 42 → 32 (chronically low — atherosclerosis risk accumulating)
- LDL has risen 120 → 142 (gradual but persistent)
- ApoB is elevated — more concerning than LDL-C alone
- A1c has drifted 5.1 → 5.6 (GH contributing — approaching pre-diabetic)
- HCT has crept to 52% (needs intervention)
- BP is gradually rising
- hs-CRP trending up (chronic low-grade inflammation)
- Homocysteine elevated (B-vitamin insufficiency)

THIS IS THE PATTERN THAT KILLS LONG-TERM USERS:
- No single lab is an EMERGENCY
- But the CUMULATIVE DRIFT over 5 years is building cardiovascular damage
- This is how bodybuilders have heart attacks at 45-50

INTERVENTIONS (Year 5+):
1. Pitavastatin 2mg/day (lipid rescue without glucose penalty — A1c already 5.6)
2. Berberine 500mg 2x/day (address A1c drift from GH)
3. Methylated B-complex (B12, folate, B6) for homocysteine
4. TMG 1500mg/day for homocysteine support
5. Blood donation for HCT 52%
6. Telmisartan 40mg for BP trending up
7. Fish oil 3g/day for CRP/inflammation
8. Increased cardio: 30 min Zone 2, 5x/week (was only doing 3x)
9. Ordered echocardiogram (baseline cardiac structure after 5 years of use)
10. CIMT scan (carotid artery thickness check)

ECHO RESULTS: Mild LVH (left ventricular hypertrophy) — early changes from chronic androgen use. Ejection fraction normal (55%). No valve abnormalities.

CIMT: 0.75mm (slightly above optimal but no plaque detected)

FOLLOW-UP PLAN:
- Quarterly labs with full advanced panel
- Annual echo
- CIMT every 2 years
- Consider reducing blast frequency (1x/year max, lower doses)
- Continue all cardiovascular support indefinitely

LESSON: Long-term use creates SLOW, CUMULATIVE damage that no single lab test catches as an emergency. Annual trending is essential. The athlete who pulls labs once and sees "everything is fine" but doesn't TRACK THE TREND over years is the one who gets blindsided by a cardiac event.

=== COMPOUND-SPECIFIC LAB ADD-ON TESTS ===

Beyond the base panels, add these tests based on which compounds you're running:

RUNNING TESTOSTERONE (any dose):
- E2 Sensitive, SHBG, HCT, Lipid Panel

RUNNING DHT DERIVATIVES (Primo, Mast, Anavar, Winstrol):
- Full Lipid Panel + ApoB
- PSA (men >30)
- GGT (for orals: Anavar, Winstrol)

RUNNING 19-NORS (Deca, Tren, MENT):
- Prolactin + Progesterone
- Cystatin-C + Urinalysis (kidney monitoring)
- Fasting Glucose + A1c (insulin resistance — especially Tren)
- Daily BP monitoring (home cuff)

RUNNING ORAL ANABOLICS (dbol, Anadrol, Superdrol, tbol, Halo):
- ALT, AST, GGT, Bilirubin, ALP (liver panel)
- Full Lipid Panel
- HCT (Anadrol/dbol specifically)

RUNNING GROWTH HORMONE:
- IGF-1
- Fasting Glucose + A1c + Fasting insulin
- Free T3 (GH suppresses T3 conversion)
- C-Peptide (if using insulin alongside)

RUNNING insulin:
- Daily finger-stick or CGM glucose monitoring
- A1c every 8-12 weeks
- C-Peptide every 3-6 months
- Fasting insulin + HOMA-IR

RUNNING GLP-1 AGONISTS (Semaglutide, Tirzepatide):
- A1c, Fasting Glucose
- Lipid Panel
- Amylase + Lipase (pancreatitis monitoring)
- Thyroid Panel (calcitonin if family history of thyroid cancer)

RUNNING EQ (Boldenone):
- HCT every 4-6 weeks (most potent HCT-raising injectable)
- Ferritin (if donating blood frequently)
- E2 may read falsely on some assays (EQ metabolite cross-reacts)

RUNNING HCG:
- LH may read falsely elevated (HCG cross-reacts on some LH assays)
- E2 (HCG stimulates intratesticular aromatization)
- Testicular size (clinical, not lab — HCG preserves testicular volume)

=== THE BOTTOM LINE ===

As Wendy and Bryan Antonelli write in the Precision Bloodwork Field Manual: "You can replace muscle. You can't replace kidneys. And you only get one liver. Treat them like your most valuable assets — because they are."

The templates in this guide remove the guesswork from bloodwork timing and selection. The case studies show what happens when labs guide decisions — and what happens when they don't.

Key principles:
1. ALWAYS pull baseline before starting ANYTHING
2. Mid-cycle labs at 6-8 weeks catch problems before they become emergencies
3. Track TRENDS over time — no single lab tells the full story
4. Add compound-specific tests based on what you're running
5. Budget $200-400 per lab draw — it's cheaper than an ER visit
6. Know your red flag thresholds and ACT on them without hesitation
7. Female monitoring requires DIFFERENT markers with emphasis on virilization
8. Long-term users: annual trending + cardiac imaging saves lives

The weights test your strength. The stage tests your physique. But the labs test your discipline. If you fail them, the rest won't matter.
