Appendix III — Glossary + Resources

Glossary of Key Terms
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•

19-Nors: Steroids derived from nandrolone (Tren, Deca, Ment) → impact
prolactin/progesterone.
Anabolic Window: Period post-training where nutrient uptake is maximized —
exaggerated under enhancement.
ApoB: Apolipoprotein B — the most predictive single marker for cardiovascular risk.
Cag (Cagrilintide): Amylin agonist, appetite suppression, pairs with GLP-1/GIP for
satiety.
CBC: complete blood count, measures red cells, white cells, hemoglobin, hematocrit.
CMP: Comprehensive metabolic panel, checks liver, kidney, and electrolyte health.
EPO: Erythropoietin, hormone regulating red blood cell production (influenced by
AAS).
GLP-1: Glucagon-like peptide-1, incretin hormone regulating appetite and glucose.
GIP: Glucose-dependent insulinotropic polypeptide, incretin hormone synergistic with
GLP-1.
HCT: Hematocrit, % of red blood cells in blood volume (viscosity marker).
IGF-1: insulin-like growth factor 1, surrogate marker for GH action.
Quint Stack: Retatrutide (GLP/GIP/Glucagon), GH (IGF-1), Cag (Amylin), + test base
→ 5 hormonal pathways synergized.
Recomp: Recomposition — simultaneous fat loss and muscle gain.
SHBG: Sex hormone-binding globulin, regulates free vs bound testosterone/estrogens.
Superdrol: Potent oral anabolic, extreme strength/fullness but lipid and liver toxic.
Trenbolone: Extremely potent anabolic, nutrient partitioning unmatched but high
systemic risk.

Resources for Athletes & Coaches
•
•
•
•
•
•

Think Big Bodybuilding Media (Scott McNally & team) — Practical PED and training
discussions.
Anabolic Bodybuilding Podcast (Trevor Benko) — Cycle breakdowns and
enhancement insights.
Dan Duchaine’s Underground Steroid Handbook — Historical context, still
foundational.
Examine.com — Evidence-based supplement and compound research.
PubMed.gov — Primary source for studies on GH, insulin, GLP-1, cardiovascular/liver
risk.
Private Labs Direct / Ulta Lab Tests / Marek Health — Services athletes use in the
U.S. for direct-to-consumer lab work.

Final Word

This book has taken us from the fundamentals of bloodwork → the science of compounds →
real-world transformation strategies → case studies and reference protocols.
The enhanced athlete who studies, applies, and tests — with ownership of their labs, their
stacks, their food, and their mindset — will not only build a stage-ready physique, but also
preserve health and longevity.
This is the new era of bodybuilding:
•
•
•

Precision over guesswork.
Longevity over recklessness.
Ownership over excuses.

Stay safe, make no excuses, take no shortcuts and GET AFTER IT!!!



=== FILE: SARMs Complete Reference Guide [CYCLE_GUIDE_13].txt ===

SARMS COMPLETE REFERENCE GUIDE — SELECTIVE ANDROGEN RECEPTOR MODULATORS
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Dave Crosland (ThinkBig), Skipp Hill (ThinkBig), Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle), Paul Barnett (Anabolic Bodybuilding)
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== WHAT ARE SARMS ===

Selective Androgen Receptor Modulators (SARMs) are a class of compounds that bind to androgen receptors in a tissue-selective manner. Unlike anabolic-androgenic steroids (AAS), which activate androgen receptors throughout the entire body, SARMs were designed to preferentially target muscle and bone tissue while minimizing effects on the prostate, liver, and other organs.

Key facts about SARMs:
- SARMs are NOT steroids — they are a distinct class of compounds
- They are NOT FDA-approved for human use — sold as "research chemicals"
- They occupy a legal gray area — not scheduled substances in most countries but not legal to sell for human consumption
- Less suppressive than traditional AAS but STILL suppressive to natural testosterone production
- Less research exists on long-term safety compared to testosterone, which has decades of medical data
- Quality control in the SARM market is extremely poor — contamination, mislabeling, and underdosing are rampant

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "SARMs are in this weird no-man's-land. They're not as strong as real steroids, but they still suppress your testosterone. So you're getting a fraction of the benefit with a lot of the same downsides. For most guys, if you're going to suppress yourself, you might as well use actual testosterone."

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "The problem with SARMs isn't the concept — selective androgen receptor modulation is brilliant in theory. The problem is that the products on the market are unregulated, untested, and you have no idea what you're actually putting in your body."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "I tell guys all the time — if you're serious about building muscle and you've made the decision to use performance enhancers, use something with decades of research behind it. Testosterone has been studied for 80 years. SARMs have been studied for maybe 15, and most of those studies were abandoned because the compounds didn't work well enough to get FDA approval."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "SARMs have their place. For someone who doesn't want to inject, who wants something milder, who just wants a slight edge — I get it. But you need to understand what you're actually getting into. They're not side-effect free, and they're not as effective as people on the internet claim."

=== OSTARINE (MK-2866 / Enobosarm) ===

Ostarine is the mildest SARM available and the most extensively studied. It was originally developed by GTx Inc. for the treatment of muscle wasting and osteoporosis. It is the closest any SARM has come to FDA approval, having completed Phase III clinical trials.

DOSING BY EXPERIENCE LEVEL:
- Beginner: 10-15mg/day
- Intermediate: 15-25mg/day
- Advanced: 25-30mg/day

CYCLE LENGTH: 8-12 weeks

HALF-LIFE: 24 hours (once daily dosing is sufficient)

PRIMARY USES:
- Cutting — preserves lean mass during caloric deficit
- Joint healing — Ostarine has notable benefits for joint and tendon health
- Lean mass preservation during PCT or time off AAS
- Bridge compound between steroid cycles (controversial — still mildly suppressive)
- Mild recomposition for those not ready to use AAS

SUPPRESSION PROFILE:
- Mildest suppression of all SARMs
- At 10-15mg/day for 8 weeks, many users show minimal LH/FSH suppression
- At 20-25mg+ for 12 weeks, suppression becomes more meaningful — PCT may be necessary
- Always get bloodwork at the end of cycle to assess recovery needs

EXPECTED RESULTS:
- Lean mass gain: 3-6 lbs over 8-12 weeks (in a caloric surplus)
- Strength increase: Mild but noticeable, especially on compound lifts
- Fat loss: Enhanced when combined with a caloric deficit
- Joint improvement: Many users report reduced joint pain within 2-3 weeks

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "Ostarine is the one SARM I'd say actually has a legitimate use case. If someone has a nagging joint issue and they want something to help while they're in a cut, 15mg of Ostarine for 8 weeks is pretty low risk and it does help."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "Ostarine is the Toyota Corolla of SARMs. It's boring, it's mild, but it works and it's reliable. You're not going to put on 20 pounds of muscle, but you'll hold onto what you have during a cut and your joints will feel better."

=== RAD-140 (Testolone) ===

RAD-140 is the most potent SARM available for muscle building. Developed by Radius Health, it was designed to eventually replace testosterone therapy with something more selective. In practice, RAD-140 is nearly as suppressive as low-dose testosterone while providing significantly less anabolic effect.

DOSING BY EXPERIENCE LEVEL:
- Beginner: 5-10mg/day
- Intermediate: 10-15mg/day
- Advanced: 15-20mg/day

CYCLE LENGTH: 6-8 weeks (very suppressive at higher doses — shorter cycles are prudent)

HALF-LIFE: 60 hours (lonGHalf-life means it accumulates significantly)

PRIMARY USES:
- Lean mass building — the strongest SARM for hypertrophy
- Strength gains — noticeable strength increases within 2-3 weeks
- Recomposition — good ratio of muscle gain to fat loss

SUPPRESSION PROFILE:
- HIGHLY suppressive — this is not a mild compound
- At 10mg+/day, expect significant LH/FSH suppression by week 4-6
- PCT is essentially MANDATORY after any RAD-140 cycle
- Some users report suppression symptoms (low energy, low libido, flat mood) as early as week 3-4

SIDE EFFECTS:
- Hair shedding in genetically predisposed individuals (androgenic activity at the scalp)
- Liver stress — elevated AST/ALT observed in clinical data
- Suppression-related sides: low libido, lethargy, flat mood in later weeks
- Possible increase in aggression
- Liver support is recommended: NAC 1200mg/day + TUDCA 250-500mg/day

EXPECTED RESULTS:
- Lean mass gain: 6-10 lbs over 8 weeks (some water included)
- Strength increase: Significant — 10-15% on major lifts
- Noticeable muscle fullness and vascularity

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "RAD-140 is the one that makes me scratch my head. It suppresses you almost as much as 200mg of test per week, but it doesn't build nearly as much muscle. If you're going to shut yourself down, why not just run actual testosterone and get twice the results?"

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "RAD-140 is the strongest SARM, no question. But 'the strongest SARM' is still weaker than a mild steroid cycle. And it'll still shut down your natural test. You're going to need PCT either way. At that point, what are you really saving yourself from? The needle?"

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "I've seen guys come off RAD-140 and their testosterone levels are in the gutter. They're surprised because they thought SARMs were supposed to be mild. RAD-140 is not mild. If you're going to use it, treat it with the same respect you'd give a real steroid cycle — get bloodwork, have PCT ready, and don't run it longer than 8 weeks."

=== LGD-4033 (Ligandrol) ===

LGD-4033 is a strong mass-building SARM developed by Ligand Pharmaceuticals. It sits between Ostarine and RAD-140 in terms of potency. It's notable for producing more water retention than other SARMs, giving it a slightly different profile.

DOSING BY EXPERIENCE LEVEL:
- Beginner: 5mg/day
- Intermediate: 5-10mg/day
- Advanced: 10-15mg/day

CYCLE LENGTH: 8-12 weeks

HALF-LIFE: 24-36 hours (once daily dosing is sufficient)

PRIMARY USES:
- Bulking — the preferred SARM for pure mass gain
- Strength gains — noticeable strength increases
- Off-season lean mass building

SUPPRESSION PROFILE:
- Moderately to highly suppressive
- Clinical studies show dose-dependent suppression of total Testosterone, LH, FSH, and SHBG
- At 10mg+/day, PCT is recommended
- At 5mg/day for 8 weeks, some users recover naturally but bloodwork should confirm

SIDE EFFECTS:
- Water retention — more than other SARMs, similar to mild AAS
- Suppression-related sides at higher doses and longer cycles
- Some users report mild headaches in the first week
- Possible lipid impact (reduced HDL)

EXPECTED RESULTS:
- Lean mass gain: 5-8 lbs over 8 weeks (more with water retention)
- Strength increase: Moderate — 5-10% on major lifts
- Fuller, rounder muscle appearance due to water retention

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "LGD is the bulking SARM. If you're going to use a SARM and you want to put on size, LGD is the move. It holds more water than something like Ostarine, so you'll look bigger on cycle, but some of that is going to come off when you stop."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "LGD-4033 gives you a decent amount of mass for a SARM. You'll hold some water, which people don't expect from SARMs, but it does fill you out. Just know that when you come off, you'll lose the water and probably 30-40% of the weight you gained."

=== MK-677 (Ibutamoren) ===

MK-677 is NOT a SARM. It is a growth hormone secretagogue (GHS) — an orally active compound that stimulates the pituitary gland to release more growth hormone by mimicking the ghrelin receptor. It is grouped with SARMs because it's sold through the same vendors and marketed alongside them.

DOSING:
- Standard dose: 10-25mg/day
- Best taken at night before bed (improves sleep quality, aligns with natural GH pulse)
- Can be run indefinitely — it is NOT suppressive to testosterone

CYCLE LENGTH: Can be used long-term (months to years). No need to cycle on and off since it does not affect testosterone production.

HALF-LIFE: 24 hours (once daily dosing)

MECHANISM:
- Mimics ghrelin — activates the growth hormone secretagogue receptor
- Increases natural GH release by 40-80%
- Raises IGF-1 levels significantly
- Does NOT directly provide exogenous GH — it stimulates your own production
- Effect plateaus after several months as the pituitary acclimates

PRIMARY USES:
- growth hormone elevation without injections
- Sleep quality improvement (dramatic improvement in deep sleep)
- Recovery enhancement between training sessions
- Appetite increase for bulking phases
- Anti-aging and skin/hair quality improvement
- Stacks well with SARMs without adding suppression

SIDE EFFECTS:
- Hunger increase — significant and sometimes overwhelming (ghrelin mimetic)
- Water retention — puffy face, bloated appearance, especially early on
- Blood sugar elevation — MK-677 can raise fasting glucose and reduce insulin sensitivity
- Lethargy/drowsiness — especially the first few weeks
- Numbness/tingling in extremities (related to GH elevation)

CRITICAL: Monitor fasting blood glucose regularly. MK-677 can push pre-diabetic individuals into diabetic blood sugar ranges. Berberine 500mg 2x/day or Metformin 500mg/day can counteract glucose effects.

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "MK-677 is the one compound from the SARM world that I actually think has good value. It's not a SARM, it boosts your GH, it makes you sleep like a baby, and it doesn't suppress your testosterone. The hunger can be brutal though — if you're cutting, it's probably not the right time."

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "I like MK-677 for guys who want the benefits of growth hormone but can't afford actual GH injections. You're not going to get the same level of GH elevation, but for the price, it's solid. Just watch your blood sugar."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "MK-677 is the easiest compound to recommend because it has the best risk-to-reward ratio. It doesn't mess with your testosterone, it improves your sleep, it helps recovery. The only people who need to be careful are those with blood sugar issues — get your fasting glucose checked before you start."

=== YK-11 ===

YK-11 is one of the most controversial compounds in the SARM category. There is genuine debate among researchers about whether it is actually a SARM or a synthetic steroid with steroidal structure. It is classified by some as a myostatin inhibitor — meaning it may block the protein (myostatin) that limits how much muscle your body can build.

DOSING BY EXPERIENCE LEVEL:
- Beginner: 5mg/day
- Intermediate: 5-10mg/day
- Advanced: 10-15mg/day

CYCLE LENGTH: 6-8 weeks MAXIMUM (potentially liver toxic and highly suppressive)

PRIMARY USES:
- Myostatin inhibition (claimed — limited evidence)
- Lean mass building
- Breaking through plateaus by theoretically raising the muscle growth hormone ceiling

SUPPRESSION PROFILE:
- Very suppressive — treat this like a steroid in terms of PCT needs
- PCT is mandatory after any YK-11 cycle

SIDE EFFECTS:
- Potentially liver toxic — liver support is ESSENTIAL
- TUDCA-500mg/day + NAC 1200mg/day throughout the cycle
- Joint pain reported by some users (possibly related to DHT-like activity)
- Hair shedding possible in predisposed individuals
- Less research exists on YK-11 than any other commonly used SARM

IMPORTANT CAVEATS:
- The myostatin inhibition claim is based on very limited cell culture studies, not human trials
- YK-11's chemical structure is steroidal — it may literally be a synthetic steroid marketed as a SARM
- Less human data available than other SARMs — you are essentially experimenting on yourself
- The risk-to-reward ratio is questionable — if it's essentially a steroid, just use an actual steroid with more research behind it

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "YK-11 is the wild card. Nobody really knows exactly what it is or how it works long-term. It might be a SARM, it might be a steroid, it might inhibit myostatin. The research is thin. If you're going to be your own guinea pig, at least protect your liver and keep the cycle short."

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "The myostatin inhibition angle is interesting in theory, but the evidence isn't there yet. We've got cell studies, not human trials. If you want to use YK-11, fine, but don't expect it to remove your genetic ceiling based on some in-vitro research."

=== S-23 ===

S-23 is one of the most potent SARMs available, approaching actual steroids in terms of both anabolic effect and suppression. It was developed by GTx Inc. and has been researched as a potential male hormonal contraceptive — which tells you everything you need to know about how suppressive it is.

DOSING BY EXPERIENCE LEVEL:
- Beginner: 10mg/day
- Intermediate: 10-20mg/day
- Advanced: 20-30mg/day

CYCLE LENGTH: 6-8 weeks (extreme suppression makes longer cycles inadvisable)

PRIMARY USES:
- Lean, dry, hard physique — produces a look similar to Winstrol
- Significant strength gains
- Cutting and contest-type conditioning without injectable steroids

SUPPRESSION PROFILE:
- EXTREMELY suppressive — will shut down natural testosterone production
- Being researched as a male hormonal contraceptive (renders users temporarily infertile at effective doses)
- PCT is MANDATORY — Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks minimum
- This is not a "mild SARM" — it behaves like a strong androgen

SIDE EFFECTS:
- Complete testosterone suppression
- Temporary infertility (sperm production ceases)
- Aggression and mood changes
- Hair shedding in predisposed individuals
- Possible liver stress
- Night sweats at higher doses
- Testicular atrophy (like being on steroids)

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "S-23 is the SARM that proves the whole point I make about SARMs. This compound will shut you down completely. Your testosterone goes to zero. Your fertility goes to zero. You need full PCT afterward. At that point, you might as well just use testosterone and Winstrol — you'd get better results with more research behind the compounds."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "S-23 is a steroid in everything but name. It shuts you down, it affects your fertility, it gives you the dry hard look. If you're going through all of that, just use the real thing. At least with testosterone plus Masteron or Winstrol you know what you're putting in your body."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "S-23 is not for beginners. Full stop. If you're considering S-23, you should honestly just consider doing a real steroid cycle instead. The suppression is identical, the sides are similar, and the results would be significantly better on actual AAS."

=== SARM STACKS ===

Combining SARMs can enhance results but also compounds suppression and potential side effects. These are the most common combinations with experience-appropriate dosing.

BEGINNER STACK — MILD ENHANCEMENT:
- Ostarine 15mg/day + MK-677 15mg/day
- Duration: 8 weeks
- Suppression: Mild (Ostarine only — MK-677 does not suppress)
- PCT: Likely not needed, but get bloodwork at week 8 to verify
- Best for: First-time SARM users wanting lean mass and improved recovery
- Expected results: 4-6 lbs lean mass, improved sleep, better joint health

CUTTING STACK:
- Ostarine 20mg/day + Cardarine (GW-501516) 15mg/day
- Duration: 8 weeks
- Suppression: Mild-moderate (Ostarine only — Cardarine is not a SARM and does not suppress)
- PCT: Get bloodwork to assess
- Note: Cardarine is a PPARdelta agonist — it enhances fat oxidation and endurance. It is NOT a SARM.
- Best for: Cutting while preserving muscle, improving cardio performance
- Expected results: Significant fat loss with muscle preservation, noticeable cardio improvement

BULKING STACK:
- LGD-4033 10mg/day + MK-677 20mg/day
- Duration: 8 weeks
- Suppression: Moderate (LGD only — MK-677 does not suppress)
- PCT: Recommended (Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks)
- Best for: Maximum mass gain from SARMs, off-season bulk
- Expected results: 6-10 lbs mass (some water), significant strength gains, enhanced recovery

RECOMP STACK:
- RAD-140 10mg/day + MK-677 15mg/day
- Duration: 8 weeks
- Suppression: High (RAD-140 is very suppressive)
- PCT: Required (Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks)
- Best for: Simultaneous muscle gain and fat loss
- Expected results: Noticeable body recomposition, 5-8 lbs lean mass, strength increases

ADVANCED STACK — HIGH SUPPRESSION:
- RAD-140 15mg/day + LGD-4033 5mg/day + MK-677 20mg/day
- Duration: 6-8 weeks (shorter due to combined suppression)
- Suppression: VERY HIGH — combining two suppressive SARMs compounds the shutdown
- PCT: MANDATORY (Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks)
- Best for: Experienced SARM users wanting maximum SARM-based results before considering AAS
- Expected results: 8-12 lbs mass, significant strength, improved recovery from MK-677
- NOTE: At this level of suppression and complexity, most experts recommend just using actual testosterone

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "When you start stacking multiple SARMs together, you're getting into the territory where your natural testosterone is going to get hammered. Two suppressive SARMs stacked together isn't 'SARM suppression' anymore — it's shutdown. If you're going to do that to yourself, do a real cycle."

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "Stacking Ostarine with MK-677 is about as far as I'd go before telling someone to just use testosterone. It's mild enough that most guys recover fine. Once you start stacking RAD-140 with LGD, you're in steroid-level suppression territory without steroid-level results."

=== SARMS FOR WOMEN ===

Women considering SARMs need to understand that even compounds marketed as "selective" still carry androgenic risk. The female body is far more sensitive to androgens than the male body.

SAFE OPTIONS FOR WOMEN:

OSTARINE — The safest SARM for women:
- Dose: 5-10mg/day
- Cycle length: 6-8 weeks
- Minimal virilization risk at these doses
- Good for lean mass preservation during cuts
- Joint health benefits
- Start at 5mg and assess for 2 weeks before increasing

MK-677 — Safe for women:
- Dose: 10-15mg/day
- Not a SARM, not androgenic — no virilization risk
- Improves sleep, recovery, and GH levels
- Can be run long-term
- Monitor blood sugar

NOT RECOMMENDED FOR WOMEN:

RAD-140 — TOO ANDROGENIC:
- Significant virilization risk even at low doses
- Androgenic effects at the scalp and skin translate to virilization in women
- Voice deepening, facial hair growth, clitoral enlargement are all possible
- There are no "safe" doses of RAD-140 for women established in research

LGD-4033 — NOT RECOMMENDED:
- Moderately androgenic
- Suppresses female hormonal axis
- Virilization risk is meaningful at any effective dose
- Insufficient safety data in women

S-23 and YK-11 — NEVER FOR WOMEN:
- Extremely androgenic
- S-23 acts as a potent androgen — will cause rapid virilization
- YK-11 has steroidal structure with unknown androgenic potency in women
- These compounds will masculinize female users

BETTER ALTERNATIVE: Many experts recommend women consider Anavar (Oxandrolone) over SARMs entirely. Anavar has decades of medical data in female patients, a well-understood virilization threshold, and more predictable results.

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "For women, I'd rather they use 5mg of Anavar than any SARM besides Ostarine. Anavar has been used medically in women for decades. We know the safety profile. With SARMs, we're guessing. Ostarine at 5-10mg is reasonable for women who don't want to use actual steroids, but that's where I draw the line."

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "Ladies, if you want to use a SARM, Ostarine is really the only option. And honestly, 5mg of Anavar is going to give you better results with a better-known safety profile. MK-677 is fine for women since it's not androgenic — just watch the appetite and blood sugar."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "Women should stick with what we know works and what we know is safe at the right doses. Anavar at 5-10mGHas been studied. Ostarine at 5-10mGHas some data. Everything else in the SARM world is a gamble for female users."

=== SARMS VS STEROIDS — HONEST COMPARISON ===

This section exists because the most common question about SARMs is whether they're a viable alternative to traditional AAS. Here's the honest assessment from experts who have experience with both.

MUSCLE BUILDING:
- SARMs are WEAKER than real steroids for building muscle — significantly weaker
- The strongest SARM cycle (RAD-140 + LGD stacked) produces roughly the results of a conservative testosterone-only cycle (300mg/week)
- A basic 500mg/week testosterone cycle will outperform any SARM stack

SUPPRESSION:
- SARMs still suppress natural testosterone production
- RAD-140 and S-23 suppress almost as much as exogenous testosterone
- If you're going to suppress your HPTA regardless, you get more bang for your buck with actual AAS
- The "SARMs are less suppressive" argument only holds for Ostarine at low doses

SAFETY RESEARCH:
- SARMs have LESS long-term safety data than testosterone
- Testosterone has been used medically since the 1930s — nearly a century of data
- Most SARMs have at best Phase I-II clinical trial data (small sample sizes, short durations)
- Several SARM development programs were abandoned by pharmaceutical companies (tells you something)
- Unknown long-term effects on organs, cardiovascular system, and cancer risk

QUALITY CONTROL:
- SARMs quality control is POOR — the market is unregulated
- Studies testing SARM products found many were mislabeled, underdosed, or contained actual prohormones/steroids
- You might be taking a real steroid without knowing it
- Testosterone from a regulated pharmacy or reputable UGL has more consistent quality

WHERE SARMS MAKE SENSE:
- People who want mild enhancement without injecting
- Bridge compound between AAS cycles (Ostarine specifically)
- Joint healing support (Ostarine specifically)
- Those not ready to commit to full AAS use but want something beyond natural
- Women who want something milder than even Anavar (Ostarine)

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "Here's my honest take on SARMs vs steroids. If you're going to suppress your testosterone — and most SARMs will — you might as well use actual testosterone. You'll get better results, more predictable side effects, decades of safety data, and it costs about the same. SARMs make sense for a very specific person: someone who wants something mild and doesn't want to inject."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "The SARM industry has done a great marketing job convincing people that SARMs are 'steroids without the side effects.' That's not true. They have side effects, they suppress you, and they build less muscle. If you've decided to cross the line into performance enhancement, cross it properly with compounds we actually understand."

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "I'm not anti-SARM. Ostarine has legitimate uses. MK-677 is great. But when guys start stacking RAD-140 with LGD and S-23 and running PCT afterward — mate, you're basically doing a steroid cycle with worse compounds. Just do the steroid cycle."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "SARMs vs steroids comes down to this: SARMs are the training wheels. They give you a taste of what enhanced performance feels like without the full commitment. But if you're serious about building a physique, at some point you're going to realize that testosterone is king for a reason."

=== SARM PCT PROTOCOLS ===

Post-cycle therapy for SARMs follows the same principles as steroid PCT — restore natural testosterone production using SERMs (Selective Estrogen Receptor Modulators) to stimulate LH and FSH release from the pituitary.

OSTARINE ONLY (Low Dose — 10-15mg/day for 8 weeks):
- May not need PCT
- Get bloodwork at week 8 — check total Testosterone, LH, FSH
- If testosterone is above 300 ng/dL and LH/FSH are in range, natural recovery is likely fine
- If suppressed, run Nolvadex 10-20mg/day for 2-4 weeks

RAD-140 (Any Dose):
- PCT is essentially mandatory
- Option A: Nolvadex (Tamoxifen) 20mg/day for 4 weeks
- Option B: Enclomiphene 12.5mg/day for 4 weeks (clener side effect profile than Nolvadex)
- Get bloodwork 4-6 weeks post-PCT to verify recovery

LGD-4033 (10mg+/day):
- PCT recommended
- Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks OR Enclomiphene 12.5mg/day for 4 weeks
- Lower doses (5mg for 8 weeks) — get bloodwork to assess need

S-23 (Any Dose):
- PCT is MANDATORY — this compound shuts down testosterone completely
- Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks minimum
- Some users may need 6 weeks of PCT for full recovery
- Consider Enclomiphene 12.5-25mg/day as an alternative SERM

YK-11 (Any Dose):
- PCT is mandatory
- Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks OR Enclomiphene 12.5mg/day for 4 weeks

STACKED SARMs:
- Any stack of two suppressive SARMs requires PCT
- Nolvadex 20mg/day for 4 weeks is the standard
- Enclomiphene 12.5mg/day for 4 weeks is the modern preferred option
- Get bloodwork 4-6 weeks post-PCT to verify hormone recovery

MK-677:
- Does NOT require PCT — it does not affect testosterone production
- Can be continued during and after SARM PCT without issue
- Actually beneficial during PCT as the GH elevation supports recovery

POST-PCT BLOODWORK (CRITICAL):
- total Testosterone
- free Testosterone
- LH (LuteinizinGHormone)
- FSH (Follicle StimulatinGHormone)
- Estradiol (sensitive assay)
- SHBG
- CBC (complete blood count)
- Liver enzymes (AST/ALT) — especially if you used YK-11 or RAD-140

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "Don't skip PCT because you used SARMs instead of steroids. Your testosterone doesn't care what compound suppressed it — suppressed is suppressed. Get your bloodwork, run your Nolvadex or Enclomiphene, and verify you've recovered before thinking about another cycle."

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "The PCT protocol for SARMs is the same as for a mild steroid cycle. Nolvadex 20mg a day for four weeks will cover most SARM cycles. Enclomiphene is even better if you can get it — clener compound, fewer side effects, does the same job."

=== SARM QUALITY AND TESTING ===

The SARM market is one of the most unregulated supplement markets in existence. Multiple independent studies have tested commercial SARM products and found alarming results.

THEQUALITY PROBLEM:
- A 2017 study (JAMA) tested 44 SARM products sold online
- Only 52% actually contained a SARM
- 39% contained another unapproved drug (often prohormones or actual steroids)
- 25% contained substances not listed on the label
- 9% contained NO active compound at all
- Many products were underdosed by 50% or more

WHAT THIS MEANS:
- The SARM you bought might not be a SARM at all
- It might be a prohormone or oral steroid (which would explain why some people get "amazing SARM results")
- If your "SARM" feels like a steroid, it might literally be one — prohormone contamination is common
- You could be unknowingly taking liver-toxic compounds without appropriate liver support
- Drug test implications — contaminated SARMs have caused positive steroid tests in tested athletes

HOW TO VERIFY:
- Third-party analytical testing is the gold standard
- Janoshik Analytical (public.janoshik.com) provides independent compound verification
- Send a sample of your product for testing, or buy from vendors who publish Janoshik test results
- Verify test results are authentic at verify.janoshik.com
- Reputable SARM vendors publish full third-party lab results for every batch
- If a vendor won't show you lab results, don't buy from them

SIGNS YOUR SARM IS CONTAMINATED:
- Results that seem "too good" for the compound — if your Ostarine cycle gives you 15 lbs, something else is in there
- Unexpected side effects: significant liver stress on Ostarine, gyno symptoms on non-aromatizing SARMs
- Rapid water retention beyond what the SARM should produce
- Positive steroid drug test results

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "The SARM market is the Wild West. You have no idea what you're actually buying. At least with testosterone, you can test it. A lot of these SARM companies are selling methylated prohormones relabeled as SARMs. That's why some guys get great results and then wonder why their liver enzymes are through the roof."

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "If you're going to use SARMs, spend the money on a Janoshik test. Send your product in and find out what's actually in it. You'd be shocked how many SARMs test positive for compounds that aren't even listed on the label. Some of the most popular SARM brands have failed multiple independent tests."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "Quality control is the number one issue with SARMs. I've seen lab results where a product labeled as Ostarine was actually a methylated prohormone. The guy taking it thought he was on a mild SARM and was actually running an oral steroid without any liver protection. That's dangerous. Always verify what you're putting in your body."

For personalized SARM protocols, cycle design, PCT planning, and guidance on transitioning from SARMs to properly managed AAS cycles — consult with the experts at Forged by Freedom (forgedbyfreedom.org). Their coaches can design a protocol tailored to YOUR goals, experience level, and health profile, whether you want to stay in the SARM lane or are ready to make the move to real testosterone. Accurate information and personalized guidance save you from wasted money, unnecessary side effects, and preventable health risks.


=== FILE: Testosterone Compound Ratios and Experience-Tiered Dosing [CYCLE_GUIDE_11].txt ===

TESTOSTERONE COMPOUND RATIOS AND EXPERIENCE-TIERED DOSING
Expert Sources: Scott McNally (ThinkBig), Dave Crosland (ThinkBig), Skipp Hill (ThinkBig), Dave Palumbo (RXMuscle), Paul Barnett (Anabolic Bodybuilding)
Reference Guide for Forged by Freedom AI Coach

=== WHY TESTOSTERONE:COMPOUND RATIOS MATTER ===

Every steroid cycle revolves around testosterone as the base. The ratio of testosterone to secondary compounds directly affects estrogen management, prolactin control, sexual function, mood, and overall side effect profile. Getting the ratio wrong is the #1 reason intermediate users have bad cycle experiences.

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "Most guys who come to me with problems on cycle, it's a ratio issue. They're running too much of one compound relative to their test base. Fix the ratio and 90% of the problems go away."

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "Testosterone is your foundation. Every other compound is built on top of that foundation. If the foundation is wrong, the whole structure is unstable."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "I always tell guys — testosterone is king. You can run whatever else you want, but if your test base isn't right, you're going to have issues with libido, mood, energy, everything."

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "The ratio between your testosterone and secondary compounds is everything. It determines how you feel, how you perform in the gym, and how you perform in the bedroom."

The biological reasons ratios matter:
- ESTROGEN (E2): Testosterone aromatizes to estradiol. Some compounds (EQ, Primo, Masteron) have anti-estrogenic properties that can crash E2 if test is too low. Others (Deca, dbol) compound estrogenic/progestogenic load.
- PROLACTIN: 19-nor compounds (Nandrolone, Trenbolone) elevate prolactin. Adequate testosterone helps maintain dopaminergic tone to counterbalance prolactin. Low test + high 19-nors = prolactin disaster.
- SEXUAL FUNCTION: Testosterone is the primary driver of libido and erectile function. When secondary compounds dominate, sexual side effects are the first sign the ratio is off.
- DHT BALANCE: Testosterone converts to DHT, which is critical for mood, confidence, and sexual function. Running very low test with DHT-derived compounds (Masteron, Anavar, Winstrol) can still feel good, but running low test with non-DHT compounds often feels terrible.

=== EXPERIENCE LEVEL DEFINITIONS ===

These tiers determine appropriate dosing ranges. Be honest about where you are — running advanced doses with beginner experience is how people get hurt.

BEGINNER (0-1 completed cycles):
- Has run testosterone only or test + one mild compound
- Still learning estrogen management and injection technique
- Has baseline bloodwork and at least one mid-cycle panel
- Should prioritize learning compound response over maximizing doses
- Typical total weekly AAS load: 300-500mg

INTERMEDIATE (2-4 completed cycles):
- Understands personal estrogen conversion rate and AI needs
- Has successfully managed side effects across multiple cycles
- Familiar with PCT and hormone recovery
- Has used at least 2-3 different compounds
- Typical total weekly AAS load: 500-1000mg

ADVANCED (5+ completed cycles):
- Extensive experience with multiple compounds and combinations
- Full bloodwork history documentinGHealth markers across cycles
- Understands personal response to every major compound class
- May be competitive bodybuilder or serious strength athlete
- Typical total weekly AAS load: 1000-2000mg+

Scott McNally: "I don't care how much research you've done. If you haven't completed at least two cycles, you're still a beginner in terms of how your body handles these compounds. The reading doesn't replace the experience."

=== TESTOSTERONE:NANDROLONE (DECA / NPP) RATIOS ===

Nandrolone (Deca Durabolin / NPP) is the classic mass builder. The #1 issue users face is "deca dick" — erectile dysfunction and loss of libido caused by nandrolone's progesterone and prolactin activity. The testosterone:nandrolone ratio is THE most important ratio to get right.

WHY THE RATIO MATTERS:
- Nandrolone is a 19-nor steroid that converts to a weaker androgen (DHN) rather than DHT
- Without adequate DHT from testosterone, sexual function suffers dramatically
- Nandrolone elevates prolactin, which suppresses libido and can cause lactation
- Higher testosterone provides more DHT and estradiol to maintain sexual function
- The ratio determines whether you feel great or miserable on this stack

BEGINNER (first nandrolone cycle):
- Testosterone: 500mg/week | Nandrolone: 300mg/week
- Ratio: 1.67:1 (test:nandrolone)
- Higher ratio gives maximum protection against deca dick
- Use NPP (short ester) for first nandrolone run — clears faster if sides appear
- NPP half-life: 2.5-3 days vs Deca half-life: 6-12 days
- Cabergoline: 0.25mg 2x/week on hand

INTERMEDIATE:
- Testosterone: 500-600mg/week | Nandrolone: 400-500mg/week
- Ratio: 1.2:1 (test:nandrolone)
- You know your prolactin response, can run compounds closer together
- Can use either NPP or Deca depending on preference
- Cabergoline: 0.25-0.5mg 2x/week as needed based on bloods

ADVANCED:
- Testosterone: 500-750mg/week | Nandrolone: 400-600mg/week
- Ratio: 1.25:1 (test:nandrolone)
- Experienced users may tolerate 1:1 but 1.25:1 is the gold standard
- Some advanced users run Deca higher than test successfully — but this is individual and requires prolactin management mastery
- Cabergoline: dose based on bloodwork, typically 0.5mg 2x/week

Scott McNally on Blood Sweat and Gear: "Keep your test higher than your Deca. I've seen so many guys come to me with limp dick because they ran 300 test and 600 Deca. That's backwards. Your test should always be equal to or higher than your nandrolone."

Dave Crosland: "The 1.25:1 ratio works for most experienced guys. Enough Deca to build serious mass, enough test to keep everything working downstairs. If you're new to Deca, bump that ratio up even more — go 1.5:1 or higher."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "I recommend test always higher than Deca. Classic ratio is about 1.25 to 1 or even 1.5 to 1. Your test is your foundation for libido, mood, everything."

deca dick PREVENTION PROTOCOL:
- Keep test:nandrolone ratio at 1.25:1 or higher
- Cabergoline 0.25-0.5mg twice weekly for prolactin control
- Proviron 25-50mg/day if sexual function still suffers (boosts free Test and DHT)
- Get prolactin levels checked at week 4-6
- If prolactin >20 ng/mL, increase cabergoline
- If still having issues, increase testosterone by 100mg/week

=== TESTOSTERONE:TRENBOLONE RATIOS ===

Trenbolone is the most powerful and controversial steroid. The test:tren ratio is hotly debated among experts with two distinct schools of thought.

WHY THE RATIO MATTERS:
- Trenbolone is a 19-nor compound — elevates prolactin like nandrolone
- Tren does NOT aromatize, but its progestogenic activity can amplify estrogenic symptoms from testosterone
- Higher testosterone = more aromatization = more potential E2 issues on top of Tren's sides
- Lower testosterone = less E2, drier look, but some users report worse libido and mood
- The "right" ratio is genuinely individual — no universal answer exists

SCHOOL 1: HIGH TEST / LOW TREN
- Testosterone: 500mg/week | Trenbolone: 200-300mg/week
- Ratio: ~2:1 (test:tren)
- Better libido and mood for most users
- More water retention, need AI for estrogen management
- Easier transition if you need to drop Tren due to sides

Scott McNally: "I prefer test higher than tren for most guys. You feel better, your libido stays, and the gains are still incredible. 500 test, 200-300 tren is a fantastic cycle."

SCHOOL 2: LOW TEST / HIGH TREN
- Testosterone: 200-300mg/week | Trenbolone: 300-500mg/week
- Ratio: ~1:1.5 (test:tren — tren dominant)
- Drier, harder look with less water
- Less estrogen to manage — some run no AI
- Higher risk of Tren mental sides and prolactin issues
- Must manage prolactin aggressively with cabergoline

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "Some guys do great on low test, high tren. They get that grainy, hard look without the water. But you have to manage your prolactin and you have to be honest about how tren makes you feel mentally."

EXPERIENCE-TIERED TREN DOSING:

BEGINNER (first tren cycle):
- Testosterone: 300-500mg/week | Trenbolone Acetate: 150-200mg/week
- ALWAYS use Trenbolone Acetate (short ester) for first tren cycle
- Tren Acetate half-life: 1-2 days — clears fast if you have bad sides
- Start LOW (150mg/week) and assess for 2-3 weeks before increasing
- This is a powerful combination even at low doses
- Cabergoline: 0.25mg 2x/week, have more on hand

INTERMEDIATE:
- Testosterone: 400-500mg/week | Trenbolone: 200-400mg/week
- Can use Acetate or Enanthate ester based on injection preference
- Tren Enanthate half-life: 5-7 days
- Adjust test:tren ratio based on which school you respond better to
- Cabergoline: 0.25-0.5mg 2x/week

ADVANCED:
- Testosterone: 300-750mg/week | Trenbolone: 400-700mg/week
- Full flexibility on ratio — you know your body's response
- Many advanced users run Tren Enanthate for fewer injections
- Prolactin management is well-dialed at this level
- Cabergoline: dose based on bloodwork

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "Trenbolone is the most powerful steroid we have. But if it's your first time, keep it at 200mg a week with Acetate and assess. You can always add more. You can't take it back once it's in your system."

=== TESTOSTERONE:EQUIPOISE (BOLDENONE) RATIOS ===

Equipoise presents a unique ratio challenge because it has ANTI-estrogenic properties. EQ can crash estrogen if you run too much relative to testosterone, which is the opposite problem of most compounds.

WHY THE RATIO MATTERS:
- EQ converts to a metabolite (1,4-dienedione) that acts as a mild aromatase inhibitor
- RunninGHigh EQ with moderate test can crash estrogen — causing joint pain, low libido, depression, anxiety
- You may need MORE testosterone with EQ than with other compounds
- Some users need ZERO AI on a test + EQ cycle because EQ handles estrogen management naturally
- The ratio must account for EQ's E2-lowering properties

BEGINNER:
- Testosterone: 500mg/week | Equipoise: 400mg/week
- Ratio: 1.25:1 (test:EQ)
- Conservative EQ dose — still plenty for appetite enhancement and vascularity
- DO NOT take an AI unless estrogen symptoms are confirmed with bloodwork
- EQ half-life: 14 days — takes 6-8 weeks to fully saturate

INTERMEDIATE:
- Testosterone: 600mg/week | Equipoise: 600mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1 (test:EQ)
- At 1:1, most users find a good balance — EQ controls some E2 naturally
- If E2 crashes (dry joints, no libido, anxiety), increase test or drop EQ dose
- Minimal or no AI needed for most users at this ratio

ADVANCED:
- Testosterone: 600-750mg/week | Equipoise: 600-900mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1 to 1:1.2 (slight EQ dominance acceptable)
- Advanced users know their aromatization rate and can adjust
- Some high aromatizers can run EQ dominant (1:1.5) because they produce excess estrogen naturally
- Run EQ for minimum 16 weeks, ideally 20 — it takes time to show its full effects

Scott McNally on ThinkBig: "EQ is great for a lean bulk. But watch your estrogen — EQ can act like a mild AI. Some guys crash their E2 without realizing it's the EQ doing it. Keep the ratio at 1:1 or test slightly higher and you'll be fine."

Dave Crosland: "With Equipoise, the biggest mistake is crashing estrogen and not knowing why you feel like garbage. Your joints hurt, your mood tanks, libido disappears. Back off the EQ or bump up the test."

EQ-SPECIFIC CONSIDERATIONS:
- Monitor hematocrit — EQ significantly raises red blood cell count
- Donate blood if hematocrit >52%
- The appetite increase from EQ is dramatic — beneficial for bulking, problematic for cutting
- EQ anxiety is a real side effect at higher doses — usually means E2 is too low
- Run for minimum 16 weeks due to very long ester. 20 weeks is ideal.

=== TESTOSTERONE:PRIMOBOLAN RATIOS ===

Primobolan (Methenolone Enanthate) is often called "The Gentleman's Compound" for its mild side effect profile. Like EQ, Primo has mild anti-estrogenic properties that affect ratio planning.

WHY THE RATIO MATTERS:
- Primo has weak anti-estrogenic activity — can reduce total estrogen slightly
- Not as aggressive as EQ at lowering E2, but still a factor at higher doses
- Primo requires higher doses to produce meaningful muscle-building results (400-800mg minimum)
- The cost of Primo means most users run it at meaningful doses — which means the anti-E2 effect is real
- Primo does NOT aromatize and is not liver toxic

BEGINNER:
- Testosterone: 400-500mg/week | Primobolan: 400mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1 (test:Primo)
- Good starting point — Primo's anti-E2 effect is mild at this dose
- AI usually not needed
- Primo Enanthate half-life: 7-10 days

INTERMEDIATE:
- Testosterone: 400-500mg/week | Primobolan: 600mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1.2-1.5 (Primo can run higher than test)
- Primo dominant cycles are common and well-tolerated
- Watch for E2 getting too low — add MENT or reduce Primo if needed
- Quality lean gains with very manageable sides

ADVANCED:
- Testosterone: 400-600mg/week | Primobolan: 600-1000mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1.5-2 (Primo dominant)
- Many advanced users run Primo significantly higher than test
- Arnold reportedly ran high-dose Primo as a staple
- At 800mg+ Primo, AI is almost never needed
- Primo + Test is the "feel good" cycle — excellent mood, minimal sides

Skipp Hill on It's Just Bodybuilding: "Primo and test is the cycle I'd run year-round if cost wasn't a factor. ThEQuality of gains is unmatched. You look good, you feel good, your bloodwork stays clen."

Dave Palumbo on RXMuscle: "Primobolan is the gold standard for quality lean tissue. It's expensive, but you get what you pay for. Run it higher than your test and you'll love the way you look and feel."

PRIMO-SPECIFIC CONSIDERATIONS:
- Verify sourcEQuality — Primo is the most commonly counterfeited steroid (often substituted with Masteron)
- Janoshik testing can verify compound identity for UGL sources
- Primo PIP (post-injection pain) can be significant at high concentrations — look for 100mg/mL for smoother injections
- Primo is safe enough for very long cycles (20-24 weeks)
- Excellent for lean bulking and cutting — versatile compound

=== TESTOSTERONE:MASTERON RATIOS ===

Masteron (Drostanolone) is the premier cosmetic/cutting compound. It's a DHT derivative that provides hardening, drying, and mild anti-estrogenic effects. Most effective at lower body fat percentages.

WHY THE RATIO MATTERS:
- Masteron is a DHT derivative — it does not aromatize and has anti-estrogenic properties
- Acts as a mild AI by binding to aromatase enzyme and SHBG
- Increases free Testosterone by displacing test from SHBG
- Best results when body fat is below 15% — the cosmetic effects (hardness, striations) require leanness to see
- Above 15% body fat, the visual effects are largely wasted

BEGINNER:
- Testosterone: 400-500mg/week | Masteron: 300-400mg/week
- Ratio: 1.25:1 (test:Masteron)
- Good first experience with Masteron's hardening effects
- Monitor hair if genetically predisposed to MPB — Masteron accelerates hair loss
- Masteron Enanthate half-life: 7-10 days. Propionate half-life: 1-2 days.

INTERMEDIATE:
- Testosterone: 400-500mg/week | Masteron: 400-500mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1 (test:Masteron)
- Excellent cutting/recomp cycle
- AI often unnecessary at this ratio — Masteron handles estrogen
- Great "feel good" factor — improved confidence, libido, mood from DHT activity

ADVANCED:
- Testosterone: 300-500mg/week | Masteron: 500-700mg/week
- Ratio: 1:1.2-1.4 (Masteron can run slightly higher)
- Pre-contest stack staple — extreme hardness and definition
- Can run Masteron dominant if you're a high aromatizer
- Stack with Tren for the ultimate contest prep look

Dave Crosland: "Masteron is one of those compounds that makes everything look better. But you have to be lean enough to see it. Under 15% body fat, Masteron makes you look like a statue. Over 15%, you're wasting your money."

HAIR LOSS WARNING:
- Masteron is one of the harshest compounds for hair loss
- DHT-derived — directly accelerates male pattern baldness in genetically susceptible individuals
- Finasteride does NOT work to protect hair from Masteron (finasteride only blocks 5-alpha reductase, which Masteron bypasses)
- If hair is a priority, consider Primobolan instead — similar benefits with less hair impact
- Topical RU58841 may offer some protection but results vary

=== TESTOSTERONE:ANAVAR (OXANDROLONE) RATIOS ===

Anavar is the mildest and most versatile oral steroid. It's the gold standard for female use and an excellent addition to male cycles for cutting, lean bulking, and strength.

WHY THE RATIO MATTERS:
- Anavar does not aromatize — no estrogen conversion
- Mild on lipids compared to most orals (but still impacts them — monitor bloodwork)
- Very mild liver toxicity — 6-8 week runs are standard, some run up to 12 weeks
- Anavar half-life: 9 hours — take in 2 divided doses
- Primarily adds strength and lean, dry mass without water
- Does not significantly impact the test:E2 balance

MALE DOSING BY EXPERIENCE:

BEGINNER:
- Testosterone: 300-500mg/week | Anavar: 30-40mg/day
- Anavar as a finisher or kickstart to a test cycle
- 6-8 weeks of Anavar within a 12-16 week test cycle
- Great introduction to oral steroids with manageable sides

INTERMEDIATE:
- Testosterone: 400-500mg/week | Anavar: 50-60mg/day
- Can run Anavar 6-8 weeks
- Stack with other injectables (Primo, Masteron) for enhanced cutting
- TUDCA 250-500mg/day for liver support

ADVANCED:
- Testosterone: 400-600mg/week | Anavar: 60-100mg/day
- Higher Anavar doses for contest prep or extreme cutting
- Can stack Anavar with Winstrol for maximum hardness (but monitor lipids closely)
- NAC 1200mg + TUDCA-500mg daily for liver protection

FEMALE DOSING (Anavar is the gold standard compound for women):

Anavar is the safest and most well-studied steroid for female use. Virilization risk is minimal at proper doses but must be monitored.

BEGINNER FEMALE:
- Anavar: 5-10mg/day (start at 5mg)
- Cycle length: 6-8 weeks
- NO testosterone base needed for females at this dose
- Monitor for virilization: voice deepening, facial hair, clitoral enlargement
- If virilization signs appear, discontinue immediately — Anavar's short half-life means sides reversEQuickly

INTERMEDIATE FEMALE:
- Anavar: 10-15mg/day
- Cycle length: 6-8 weeks
- Some females add low-dose testosterone (5-10mg/week) or Primobolan (25-50mg/week)
- Split doses AM/PM for stable blood levels

ADVANCED FEMALE:
- Anavar: 15-20mg/day (this is considered high for females)
- Cycle length: 6-8 weeks maximum
- May stack with low-dose Primobolan (50-75mg/week) for enhanced results
- Close virilization monitoring required at these doses
- Bloodwork every 4 weeks

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "For women, Anavar is it. It's the one compound I feel comfortable recommending. Start at 5mg, see how you respond, and only go up if needed. Most women see great results at 10mg."

Scott McNally: "Anavar is the safest oral for both men and women. For guys, 50mg a day added to a test base gives you noticeably more strength and a harder look. For women, 5-10mg is a game changer."

=== FEMALE COMPOUND SAFETY — WHAT WOMEN CAN AND CANNOT USE ===

Female compound selection is fundamentally different from male compound selection. Women are dramatically more sensitive to androgens. The wrong compound at any dose can cause permanent virilization — voice deepening, facial hair growth, clitoral enlargement, jawline changes. Some of these effects are IRREVERSIBLE even after discontinuation.

SAFE COMPOUNDS FOR WOMEN (low virilization risk at proper doses):
- Anavar (Oxandrolone): 5-20mg/day — THE gold standard female compound. Safest oral, well-studied in women. Start at 5mg.
- Primobolan (injectable): 25-75mg/week — Very mild, excellent for lean gains. Second-best option after Anavar.
- Testosterone (low dose): 5-10mg/week — Micro-dose for libido, energy, and mild anabolic effect. Use propionate ester for fast clearance.
- Nandrolone/NPP (very low dose): 25-50mg/week — Joint support + mild mass. Use NPP (short ester) so it clears fast if sides appear.

COMPOUNDS WOMEN MUST NEVER USE — EXTREME VIRILIZATION RISK:
- Anadrol (Oxymetholone): NEVER for women. Despite some outdated medical literature on low-dose use, the virilization risk is unacceptably high. Causes rapid masculinization even at low doses.
- Dianabol (Methandrostenolone): NEVER for women. Highly androgenic, heavy aromatizer, rapid virilization.
- Trenbolone (any ester): NEVER for women. The most androgenic steroid available. Will virilize at any dose.
- Winstrol (Stanozolol): NEVER for women. Despite its reputation as a "cutting" drug, it is highly androgenic and causes severe virilization — voice changes, hair loss, acne. The androgenic effects hit women fast and hard.
- Halotestin (Fluoxymesterone): NEVER for women. Extremely androgenic, extremely hepatotoxic.
- Superdrol (Methasterone): NEVER for women. Extreme androgenic potency.
- Methyltrienolone (M-Tren): NEVER for women. oral tren — most potent androgen available.

USE WITH EXTREME CAUTION (advanced female competitors only):
- Masteron (Drostanolone): 50-75mg/week MAXIMUM, only pre-contest, only for advanced competitors. DHT derivative — accelerates virilization. Must monitor weekly.
- Equipoise (Boldenone): Generally avoided for women. Long ester means if virilization appears, the compound takes weeks to clear. Androgenic metabolites accumulate.
- Turinabol: Some advanced female athletes use 5-10mg/day for 4-6 weeks. Moderate virilization risk. Not recommended as a first choice.

Paul Barnett on Anabolic Bodybuilding: "For women, Anavar is it. It's the one compound I feel comfortable recommending. Anything else and you're playing with fire. I've seen women permanently damage their voices with one cycle of Winstrol."

Scott McNally: "Ladies, stick with Anavar and maybe low-dose Primo. That's all you need. The women who get into trouble are the ones who try to use male compounds at 'lower doses' — it doesn't work that way. Your receptors are far more sensitive."

FEMALE CYCLE TEMPLATES:

FEMALE TEMPLATE 1: FIRST CYCLE (Beginner)
- Anavar: 5mg/day for 6 weeks
- No testosterone base needed
- Support: Liver support (NAC 600mg/day), multivitamin
- Expected results: Noticeable strength gains, mild muscle hardening, some fat loss
- Monitor: Voice, skin, body hair weekly

FEMALE TEMPLATE 2: LEAN GAINS (Intermediate)
- Anavar: 10mg/day (split AM/PM) for 8 weeks
- Optional: Testosterone Propionate 5mg/week (for libido and recovery)
- Support: NAC 600mg/day, fish oil
- Expected results: Visible muscle development, significant strength gains, improved body composition

FEMALE TEMPLATE 3: BIKINI/FIGURE PREP (Intermediate-Advanced)
- Anavar: 10-15mg/day for 8 weeks
- Primobolan: 50mg/week for 12 weeks
- Optional: Testosterone Propionate 5-10mg/week
- Support: NAC 600mg/day, TUDCA 250mg/day
- Expected results: Hard, lean physique with muscle fullness. Competition-ready conditioning.

FEMALE TEMPLATE 4: ADVANCED COMPETITOR
- Anavar: 15-20mg/day for 6-8 weeks
- Primobolan: 50-75mg/week for 16 weeks
- Testosterone Propionate: 10mg/week
- Support: Full liver support, bloodwork every 4 weeks
- Virilization monitoring: WEEKLY voice recordings, photo documentation
- This is the upper limit for female use. Going beyond this enters dangerous territory.

FEMALE PCT:
- Women typically do NOT need traditional PCT (no SERMs like Nolvadex or Clomid)
- After discontinuing compounds, natural hormone production resumes within 2-4 weeks
- Short esters (Anavar, NPP, test propionate) clear faster = faster recovery
- If using longer esters (Primo Enanthate), allow 3-4 weeks for full clearance
- Support recovery with: adequate sleep, stress management, proper nutrition

=== MULTI-COMPOUND RATIO TEMPLATES ===

Pre-built stacks for common goals with proper ratios. All assume MALE use unless noted — see Female Cycle Templates above for women.

TEMPLATE 1: CLASSIC MASS STACK (Intermediate)
- Testosterone: 500mg/week
- Nandrolone (Deca): 400mg/week
- Dianabol: 30-40mg/day (weeks 1-6)
- Ratios: Test:Deca = 1.25:1
- Duration: 16-20 weeks
- Support: AI as needed, Cabergoline 0.25mg 2x/week, HCG 500 IU 2x/week
- This is the time-tested mass stack that has built more muscle than any other combination

TEMPLATE 2: lean bulk STACK (Intermediate-Advanced)
- Testosterone: 400mg/week
- Primobolan: 600mg/week
- Anavar: 50mg/day (last 8 weeks)
- Ratios: Test:Primo = 1:1.5 (Primo dominant)
- Duration: 20 weeks (Anavar last 8)
- Support: AI usually not needed, HCG 500 IU 2x/week
- The "feel good" lean bulk — quality gains, great mood, excellent bloodwork

TEMPLATE 3: CUTTING STACK (Intermediate)
- Testosterone: 300mg/week
- Masteron: 400mg/week
- Anavar: 50mg/day (last 6-8 weeks)
- Ratios: Test:Mast = 1:1.3 (Masteron dominant)
- Duration: 12-16 weeks
- Support: AI rarely needed with Masteron present, HCG 500 IU 2x/week
- Best when starting below 15% body fat

TEMPLATE 4: RECOMP STACK (Intermediate-Advanced)
- Testosterone: 400mg/week
- Trenbolone Acetate: 300mg/week
- Masteron: 400mg/week
- Ratios: Test:Tren = 1.3:1, Test:Mast = 1:1
- Duration: 12-16 weeks
- Support: Cabergoline 0.25-0.5mg 2x/week, HCG 500 IU 2x/week
- The ultimate body recomposition stack — gain muscle while losing fat

TEMPLATE 5: CONTEST PREP STACK (Advanced)
- Testosterone Propionate: 200mg/week
- Trenbolone Acetate: 400mg/week
- Masteron Propionate: 500mg/week
- Anavar: 50-80mg/day (last 6 weeks)
- Winstrol: 50mg/day (last 3-4 weeks, optional)
- Ratios: Test:Tren = 1:2 (Tren dominant), Test:Mast = 1:2.5 (Mast dominant)
- Duration: 16 weeks out from show
- Support: Cabergoline, HCG until 4 weeks out, letrozole on hand for gyno flares
- This is what competitive bodybuilders run. Not for recreational use.

TEMPLATE 6: BEGINNER-FRIENDLY SECOND CYCLE
- Testosterone: 500mg/week
- Anavar: 40mg/day (weeks 1-8 or last 8 weeks)
- Ratios: N/A (Anavar doesn't affect test ratio concerns)
- Duration: 16 weeks
- Support: AI as needed, HCG 500 IU 2x/week
- Perfect second cycle — adds mild oral without complex ratio management

=== EXPERIENCE-TIERED QUICK REFERENCE TABLE ===

Compound dosing ranges by experience level. All doses assume a proper testosterone base.

TESTOSTERONE (Base):
- Beginner: 300-500mg/week
- Intermediate: 500-750mg/week
- Advanced: 500-1000mg+/week
- Notes: Always the base. Split injections 2x/week minimum.

NANDROLONE (Deca/NPP):
- Beginner: 200-300mg/week (NPP preferred)
- Intermediate: 300-500mg/week
- Advanced: 400-700mg/week
- Ratio to Test: 1.25-1.67:1 (test:nandrolone)
- Notes: Always run test higher. Cabergoline required.

TRENBOLONE (MALE ONLY — NEVER for women):
- Beginner: 150-200mg/week (Acetate ONLY)
- Intermediate: 200-400mg/week
- Advanced: 400-700mg/week
- Ratio to Test: Varies (two schools — see Tren section)
- Notes: Most powerful steroid. Manage prolactin. Cardio non-negotiable.

EQUIPOISE (Boldenone):
- Beginner: 300-400mg/week
- Intermediate: 400-600mg/week
- Advanced: 600-1000mg/week
- Ratio to Test: 1:1 to 1.25:1 (test equal or higher)
- Notes: Anti-estrogenic — may crash E2. Run 16-20 weeks. Monitor hematocrit.

PRIMOBOLAN:
- Beginner: 300-400mg/week
- Intermediate: 400-600mg/week
- Advanced: 600-1000mg/week
- Ratio to Test: 1:1 to 1:2 (Primo can run higher)
- Notes: Safest injectable. Expensive. Verify sourcEQuality (often faked).

MASTERON:
- Beginner: 300-400mg/week
- Intermediate: 400-500mg/week
- Advanced: 500-700mg/week
- Ratio to Test: 1:1 to 1:1.4 (Masteron can run slightly higher)
- Notes: Best below 15% BF. Hair loss risk. Mild anti-E2 properties.

ANAVAR (Male):
- Beginner: 30-40mg/day
- Intermediate: 50-60mg/day
- Advanced: 60-100mg/day
- Notes: Mildest oral. 6-8 week runs. Split doses. Liver support recommended.

ANAVAR (Female):
- Beginner: 5-10mg/day
- Intermediate: 10-15mg/day
- Advanced: 15-20mg/day
- Notes: Gold standard female compound. Monitor virilization. 6-8 week max.

DIANABOL (MALE ONLY — NEVER for women):
- Beginner: 20-30mg/day
- Intermediate: 30-50mg/day
- Advanced: 50-80mg/day
- Notes: Kickstart compound only (4-6 weeks). Heavy aromatizer. Liver toxic.

ANADROL (MALE ONLY — NEVER for women):
- Beginner: 25-50mg/day (start at 25mg)
- Intermediate: 50-75mg/day
- Advanced: 50-150mg/day
- Notes: Strongest oral. 4-6 week max. Does not aromatize but causes estrogenic symptoms. Liver protection essential.

WINSTROL (MALE ONLY — NEVER for women):
- Beginner: 25-30mg/day
- Intermediate: 30-50mg/day
- Advanced: 50-100mg/day
- Notes: dry compound for cutting. Harsh on joints, lipids, and hair. 4-6 week max.

TURINABOL:
- Beginner: 30-40mg/day
- Intermediate: 40-60mg/day
- Advanced: 60-80mg/day
- Notes: clen, dry gains. Moderate liver stress. Good for athletes. 6-8 weeks.

=== ADJUSTING RATIOS — READING YOUR BODY AND BLOODWORK ===

No ratio is perfect for everyone. You must learn to read the signals your body gives you and adjust accordingly.

SIGNS YOUR ESTROGEN (E2) IS TOO HIGH:
- Water retention / bloating / puffy face
- Sensitive or itchy nipples (gyno warning)
- Emotional / mood swings / crying easily
- Elevated blood pressure
- Acne flare-up
- FIX: Add or increase AI (Aromasin preferred), or reduce testosterone dose

SIGNS YOUR ESTROGEN (E2) IS TOO LOW (crashed):
- Dry, cracking joints
- Low libido / erectile dysfunction
- Depression / anxiety / flat mood
- Fatigue and lethargy
- Dry skin / dry lips
- FIX: Stop AI immediately, reduce or remove anti-estrogenic compounds (EQ, Primo, Masteron), add Dianabol 10-20mg/day as an estrogen rescue

SIGNS YOUR PROLACTIN IS TOO HIGH:
- Erectile dysfunction / inability to orgasm
- Nipple sensitivity or leaking
- Low libido despite adequate testosterone
- Brain fog / lack of motivation
- FIX: Increase Cabergoline dose, get prolactin blood test, ensure vitamin B6 (P5P form) at 100-200mg/day

SIGNS YOUR DHT IS TOO LOW:
- Low libido despite good testosterone and E2 levels
- Lack of confidence / drive / assertiveness
- Soft erections despite being on testosterone
- FIX: Add Proviron 25-50mg/day, or add a DHT derivative (Masteron at low dose)

BLOODWORK TIMING FOR RATIO ADJUSTMENT:
- Get bloodwork at week 4-6 of any new cycle or ratio change
- Key markers: total Testosterone, free Testosterone, Estradiol (sensitive assay), Prolactin, CBC (hematocrit), Lipid Panel, Liver enzymes (AST/ALT), Kidney function (BUN/Creatinine)
- Compare to your personal baseline — not internet reference ranges
- Adjust ratios based on how you FEEL combined with what bloodwork SHOWS
- Never adjust based on bloodwork alone if you feel great, and never ignore bloodwork if you feel terrible

Scott McNally: "Bloodwork doesn't lie, but your body doesn't lie either. If your bloodwork says your estrogen is in range but you feel like garbage, something else is going on. Use both data points."

Dave Crosland on Drugs N Stuff: "The guys who get the best results are the ones who keep detailed logs. How do you feel? What's your libido? How's your mood? How are your joints? Write it down every week. That's how you dial in your ratios over time."
